> **Purpose:** This folder contains ledger files for Tier A (deep) and Tier B side characters per Part 19's four-tier Depth Spectrum (A/B/C/D). Tier A and Tier B characters receive full individual ledger files using the thirteen-section Character Ledger Template, each with a registry row in this _index.md. Tier C characters are tracked as entries in this _index.md; each Tier D person is one registry row.

> **Tier Definitions (Per Part 19 Area 1.2):**
> - **Tier A:** Deep-depth side characters with substantial independent arcs, explicit psychological profiles, and narrative functions that can generate CSS-5+ events. Require full ledger files.
> - **Tier B:** Moderate-depth characters with defined roles but limited arc scope. Hold their own full ledger file (the Ledger Type reads Tier B); tracked here by a registry row that points at the file (name, function, current status, key relationships).
> - **Tier C:** Functional/background characters with minimal narrative complexity. Tracked in the index with single-line entries.
>
> **Tier D — the fourth row of the Part 19 Area 1.2 spectrum:**
> - **Tier D — Named Conversational:** one registry row in this index and nothing else, registered at Gate 2. The rules of the tier (what it carries and does not carry, speaking, promotion, negotiable canon) are Part 19 Rules 19.1.2.3 to 19.1.2.7; the row layout is Part 37 Area 2.11; the picked code is Part 39 Section 3.5. See **"Tier D Named Conversational Characters — Registry"** below.

> **Critical Rules:**
> - Mentor Death (Part 19 Rule 19.2.1.1) — a permanent event — requires immediate ledger update and [CONSEQUENCE AUDIT] C-18.
> - No unresolved Tier A character threads at arc close (Part 19 Rule 19.9.3.2).
> - CS%/TS% values for Tier A characters are tracked in their full ledger file Section 2 and summarized in the Tier A registry table here. Tier B characters include CS%/TS% in their file Section 2. All values per Part 31 Area 21.4.

> **Update Protocol:** The Pre-Write Ledger Check (`guardrails/ledger/guardrail-ledger-update-law.md`) reads the Tier A and Tier B files, the Tier C lines and the Tier D rows of every character in the Area, by name, before any prose where they appear. The Post-Write Ledger Update (`guardrails/ledger/guardrail-ledger-update-law.md`) updates entries after any narrative function event, death, status shift, or significant relationship change.

> **Marker Legend:** These markers appear in the entries of this ledger. Open cells otherwise carry `OPEN-AREA` or `OPEN-AUTHOR` (Part 37 Area 5).
> - `[ASKED]`: the author chose this value.
> - `[DERIVED]`: the drafter inferred this value from cited evidence and the author reviewed it in the digest without overriding it (Part 39 Rule 39.3.3.1); it is pending only when the entry also says flagged for author confirmation.
> - `[AUTHOR-PENDING]`: a question about this value has been raised and is not yet answered.
> - `[MARKED SLOT]`: a slot left empty on purpose until the author rules.
> - "flag, do not invent": when a scene needs the value, the prose may establish one small fact at a time under Part 28 Area 17, logged in this ledger and offered to the author as a draft value at the next Section close. A name or a major entity (Part 28 Area 20.4) goes to the author first, through the Decision Consultation Gate.

**Annotations:**

**Corin Vale** — full record in `corin-vale.md` (own file since the author directive); the registry row above points at that file per Area 2.11.

**Brannic Ost / Vaneth — Tier rationale:** a single-scene allied-kingdom liaison soldier is Tier C (Named Incidental). By author ruling, Brannic Ost and Vaneth moved from Tier B down to Tier C. The explicit future-recurrence commitment in Brannic's parting line is kept as a narrative hook and does not raise the tier. Valia's orders at the door: he lies on his back for six days, nobody tells him he may rise until she has watched him walk the length of the ward, he has broth every third hour, and the house sends to the hill if the cut so much as thinks of lifting. At the Court's morning, Year 1247, Ilovany, Day 2, he is alive in the kingdom hospital and "badly cut": "the healers closed the cut in the first week, and the wound has not taken to mending since", and the healers "will not yet say whether he stays that way" (Crowe and Kade at the Court). Qi: ***Warrior Level 1 Mid***, "low and thin, flickering like a lamp left to burn on the last of its oil" on the road, and by the Court's morning so low that Kade "could barely read" it ("like water in a well in the dry month, with the clay showing"). Mind, feeling and soul: counting and plain to the end; he gave the Captain his account before he would be lifted ("There were about twenty of them... Qi: ***Warrior Level 5 Peak***; she reads a weaker fighter through the steel (the two she fells are ***Warrior Level 3 Low*** and ***Warrior Level 3 Mid***), and Rask's pressure slowed her own qi "to a crawl". Mind, feeling and soul: she held the gap before the wounded and before the thin voice ("She heard it and did not go. The gap came first."), spared the two she felled ("Stay down"), and after the stand her voice "did not sound like her own" ("Report by pairs, and tell me plainly who is hurt."); she told herself again that it was three hurt and none dead "from a man who could have left all nine of them in the road", "because the first telling had not gone in", and she carried Roke's account to the post exactly as he gave it; at the Court's morning she is at the post with her eight (Crowe's report); nothing recorded for soul. |
| Kerra Tonkin | Side Character — Tier C (Part 19) | Chapter 004 — The Measure of a Gift (Ref: 1.1.1.004) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active — registered before drafting on 2026-10-17 (Part 39 Rule 39.4.3.1, Route 1) for Chapter Spec 1.1.1.004 and **first on the page in Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004)**, where she wins the first bout of the novel and gives Varya the first "not yet"; Part 39 PARTIAL (Tier C core-6 + 1 Signature + the age band and three L30 anchors; derived from the plan; the Signature is the planner's proposal confirmed with the Chapter Spec, and is not an `[ASKED]` value); her Signature fired on the page; Check #21 at the Gate 1 of 2026-10-18: decided by the Gate 1 checker (Tier C) as [KEEP PROFILE] (see the Chapter 004 page record below the behavioral lines) | 2026-10-18 | A pupil on the floor of Sapphire Ascent Academy: thirteen (the author's figure; the page gives no age), ***Mortal Tier 1 Blood Stage*** (the page's own string; the author's ruling of 2026-10-17). She wins the first bout, against Colan Rundle, by a changed distance and a stroke to the forearm, goes first to his wrist, and answers Varya's "Can I try?" with the floor's first "Not yet". **Gender: female (she/her), set by the page of Chapter 004** (the author's rulings had left it open) | **As of Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), 1247.1.8 / PD 8, the morning of the first day of the intake, at the end of the near bench:** Body: tired and fed up with waiting for food; Colan's wooden blade slapped across her forearm with a flat crack and she "shook the arm out and kept moving", a flap was torn from her coat, her coat is "dark at the elbows", and after the bout she sits down "like a rope let go" with "a tiredness that had waited all through the bout" come down into her shoulders; in the long bout hunger arrives all at once ("I could eat this bench, and the one behind it"). Qi: none (Mortal Tier 1, Blood stage: "Blood stage" is her own word in the salute; read as absence). Mind, feeling and soul: cheerful and quick in the ring, talking all the way; she goes to the heavier pupil's wrist "before she did anything else" ("Let me see it."), tells Varya "Not yet" "as plainly as she would have given the time, with no apology in it and no scorn", holds ***Midrib Stay*** beside her long enough for Varya to see where her feet are, re-ties and finishes the knot in the worn cord on her hilt through the second bout and the Grandmaster's speech, and says of the five's rotation that they "have drilled that change at the third ring every dawn for months"; nothing recorded for soul. |

He waits at the rail in a held stance until the ring is clear, waits for Loveday Rowse to finish a sentence before he answers it, wins the second bout by ***Foiblelid***, asks the judge which touch came first and abides by her call, and tells Aegon what carrying the first thread of qi is like. **Gender: male (he/him), set by the page of Chapter 004** (the author's rulings had left it open) | **As of Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), 1247.1.8 / PD 8, the morning of the first day of the intake, at the end of the near bench:** Body: unhurt; sits at the end of the near bench "breathing evenly and rubbing his forearm where her first cut had landed", and later sits down hard at the end of the bench at the break-up and lets his head fall back with his eyes shut. Qi: ***Senior Level 1 Mid*** (the page's own string); "a thread of pale light" runs along his point, and he spends a little qi in ***Midribstrike***, ***Leaf Pace*** and ***Foiblelid***. Mind, feeling and soul: courteous and exact; he lowers his blade to Loveday's feet only after she has finished speaking, "and then a moment longer"; after the bout his eyes go to the pale patch on her blade and not to the judge, and he asks "Grandmaster, which touch came first?", is told "Yours" and says "Then the bout is won, and I abide by the call"; asked by Aegon "What is it like to carry the first thread of qi?" he looks down at his boots, takes his time and answers "It is like carrying a full cup across a room, so that most of the time you forget it, and then you remember it all at once, and you walk more carefully", then "That is all I have, and I would give you more if I had it"; during the long bout he misses the turn of the sword, "watching the Grandmaster's hand"; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the Eighth Hall of Sapphire Ascent Academy before the first bell, where the quiet is kept, ***Senior Level 1 Mid*** (the narration: "a Senior of the first Level, middle of it"), he crosses the floor "setting each foot down and testing it before he trusted the next", with his eyes on his own feet, and they "did not lift as the Head Elder went by"; the Head Elder watches the foot that came down first. |

**Behavioral Lines (Part 39 Area 41.3 — Tier C: mandatory-core six + 1 Signature each):**

**Wenna Tarr — Behavioral (Part 39, core-6):** L01 `B39-L01-HANDS-02` — the hands find a piece of the rig and work it — a strap end, a buckle, a hub pin — and put it down finished `[DERIVED]` (from: author-locked Load-Wise Hand silhouette — her attention lives in the rig); L10 `B39-L10-FILLER-03` — with nothing to say, asks after something practical — the road, the weather, the load — asked and meant `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — she reads wagons the way other people read faces); L03 `B39-L03-CHANNEL-07` — feeling goes into work on an object: worry becomes a tightened strap, grief becomes a re-greased hub `[ASKED]` (author-locked silhouette, stated verbatim); L08 `B39-L08-REFLEX-09` — protects the team and the load before any person present; the reflex does not consult her `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — right about wagons far more often than she is about people); L29 `B39-L29-HANDS-02` — maintenance: in any wait something of the rig is cleaned, checked or oiled `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette; structural — Stonemill Crossing is a staging point where wagons stand waiting, stonemill-crossing.md) `[SIGNATURE]`; L34 origin — `B39-L34-CLASS-04` trade necessity: Sapphire Road haulage punishes a shifted load and the body complied `[DERIVED]` (structural: bar-stock and ingredient traffic through Stonemill Crossing, stonemill-crossing.md), status `B39-L34-STATUS-02` established in ledger, not yet in prose. SIGNATURE: `B39-L29-HANDS-02` — in any wait the rig gets worked: a hub pin checked, a rope re-laid, the bit rings wiped — the wagon is never idle even when she is. Origin: `B39-L34-CLASS-04` (trade necessity), `B39-L34-STATUS-02`.

**Halric Mear — Behavioral (Part 39, core-6):** L01 `B39-L01-GAZE-02` — watches the hands rather than the face, and knows what a queue is doing before it says so `[DERIVED]` (from: author-locked Name-Keeper silhouette — years of reading the muster line); L10 `B39-L10-NAME-06` — uses the name at the start of every address, a trained procedural habit `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — he remembers names and formations, years of them; structural — the muster stone processes returnees by name, rank and formation, hammer-and-hilt-gate.md) `[SIGNATURE]`; L03 `B39-L03-LATENCY-07` — the gap between feeling and showing is filled with a procedural act — the roll closed, a chain re-hung — that buys the time `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — what reaches him reaches the roll first); L08 `B39-L08-LINE-01` — will not lie in the record, whatever he will do outside it `[ASKED]` (author-locked silhouette: his conscience lives in the roll — who came through, who did not come back, who he waved past that he should not have); L29 `B39-L29-HANDS-05` — the hands go to writing: the roll, the lists, yesterday's marks squared before they are needed `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — the gate's institutional memory, kept current by hand); L34 origin — `B39-L34-CLASS-01` institutional drill: the warden service's name-rank-formation processing at the muster stone, carried out of the office and into everything `[DERIVED]` (structural: hammer-and-hilt-gate.md), status `B39-L34-STATUS-02` established in ledger, not yet in prose. **Brya Tull — Behavioral (Part 39, core-6):** L01 `B39-L01-STILL-02` — never entirely still; some small part is always in motion — heel, fingers, gaze — and it only stops when she is genuinely alarmed `[DERIVED]` (from: author-locked Gate Sparrow silhouette) `[SIGNATURE]`; L10 `B39-L10-FILLER-08` — with nothing to say, offers something: a seat, water, a hand, a place in the shade `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — the junior everyone knows and nobody minds); L03 `B39-L03-ONSET-01` — instant and total; the feeling is fully present, and showing, before the event has finished happening `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — feeling lands on her instantly and completely); L08 `B39-L08-POWERLESS-05` — learns and uses the names of everyone with no leverage — the carters and apprentices she knows by face — and expects others to `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — the people her attention escapes toward are the frightened and the small); L29 `B39-L29-SILENCE-09` — idle attention escapes sideways to the people in front of her: who looks frightened, who is lying badly, whose child is about to bolt `[ASKED]` (author-locked silhouette, stated verbatim); L34 origin — `B39-L34-CLASS-07` the body imposed it: the stillness was never in her, and the watching rides the restlessness — the drill trained everything else, not this `[DERIVED]` (from: the locked silhouette — it makes her poor at the paperwork and unexpectedly good at the actual job, so the service did not build it), status `B39-L34-STATUS-02` established in ledger, not yet in prose. SIGNATURE: `B39-L01-STILL-02` — some part of her is always moving, and the whole gate can read the moment it stops, because it only stops when something is wrong. Origin: `B39-L34-CLASS-07` (the body imposed it), `B39-L34-STATUS-02`.

**Ansel Roke.** Viewpoint holder of the opening fight, and seen from outside in the next two scenes. His level is shown as ***Warrior Level 1 Mid*** (his own thought, "the one Warrior in the mine's guard", and Tregear's reading of him), and his soul sense "ran fifty paces". His Signature `B39-L08-REFLEX-10` fired in the call ("Roke counted what he had: eight guards and himself, and one of the eight out of it already", then the horn, then the orders) and again in his plain account to Tregear. Registered lines the page shows: L01 `B39-L01-GAZE-05` (he looks back along the file at the bend, counts the file again as he walks and places his guards at the mouth of the narrow); L07 `B39-L07-COUNT-01` (the carters and the clerk, the guards, "Roke counted fourteen" birds, and "I counted the birds and did nothing with the number"); L29 `B39-L29-SILENCE-09` (his eyes on the road and the file, his thumb on the horn "and he could not have said when it had gone there"). Open lines, with the page evidence on record: L10 stays `OPEN-AREA`; he answers a practical question with a plain reason and an order ("So the lamps go up only when you reach the mouth of the narrow, not a pace before, and you walk the team in rather than drive it") and calls his guards by their given names. L03 stays `OPEN-AREA`; when Davin Tarn falls "Roke said his name once, and the ditch gave him nothing back" and he goes on, and wounded he charges himself in his account. The remaining L02 and L07 dimensions stay `OPEN-AREA`. Address forms: the text coins none (Roke uses given names, and his guards use no vocative to him), so the `OPEN-AUTHOR` address question in the pending table stands.

**Elowen Tregear.** Viewpoint holder of the stand at the wagon line. Her level is shown as ***Warrior Level 5 Peak*** ("the very top of the fifth Level") and her rank word is "Captain", used by Hasler and Gareth Sefton and by Crowe at the Court. Her Signature `B39-L08-REFLEX-04` fired in the gap: she hears the thin voice from under the fourth wagon and "did not go. The gap came first", and she sends water and stretchers only after the stand. Registered lines the page shows: L02 `B39-L02-DEFORM-02` (the four-pace gap between the third wagon's tail and the fourth wagon's nose "was the only open place between the plain coats and the last two wagons; the ditch and the trees closed everything else"); L07 `B39-L07-MODE-05` and `B39-L07-COUNT-01` ("One pair, two, three, four", "Nine veterans picked", and after the stand "Three hurt and none dead", then she "went up the file, counting" and finds the dead: "Three."); L01 `B39-L01-POSTURE-01` and L29 `B39-L29-WAIT-04` (after the stroke "She stood", let her breath come back and straightened "in the road with her saber at her side" before she called for the report by pairs). Open lines, with the page evidence on record: L10 stays `OPEN-AREA`; she gives orders as complete instructions and answers a report with an order ("Then let it split, and keep the sound half in front of your face. Nobody gives a pace unless I say so."). L03 stays `OPEN-AREA`; her voice "did not sound like her own" after the stand, and she says the count to herself a second time. The remaining L02 and L07 dimensions stay `OPEN-AREA`. Address forms: the soldiers say "Captain" and no other form is coined.

On the floor and in the Academy everyone is addressed by name, with no title (the author's ruling of 2026-10-17); no other form is coined. **Collective codes.** The floor code of the Academy's foundation yard (`ledger-worlds-location/sapphire-ascent-foundation-yard.md`, Registered 2026-10-17) and the Nine Halls code as amended; each shows conformance, not membership.

**Behavioral Lines added 2026-10-17 (Part 39 Area 41.3, Tier C: mandatory-core six + 1 Signature each, with the YC-2 band and anchors):**

**Kerra Tonkin — Behavioral (Part 39, core-6):** L01 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 03; no plan item settles her body conduct); L10 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 03; plan evidence on record: she answers Varya's "Can I try?" with "Not yet", plainly and without shame, which is the floor's custom and not a personal L10 value; no catalog code is picked); L03 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 03; no plan item settles how feeling reaches the surface); L08 `B39-L08-REFLEX-03` — reaches for the injured before the cause of the injury `[DERIVED]` `[SIGNATURE]` (from: plan, Area 03 anchor 4 and Behavioral Plan: after the bout she goes first to the heavier pupil's wrist); L29 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 04; no plan item names her waiting beat; plan evidence on record: she re-ties the worn cord on her hilt while the second bout runs, which the plan registers as an age-band anchor); L34 origin — `OPEN-AREA` (the plan gives no origin for the Signature). AGE BAND: `B39-L30-BAND-02` — Adolescent (Part 16 YC-2) `[DERIVED]` (from: age thirteen, the author's figure; Part 16 Area 16.1, ages 12 to 16). ANCHORS (Rule 39.34.1.1): `B39-L30-ANCHOR-03` — hunger, tiredness or cold arriving suddenly and completely, overriding everything (planned: tiredness after the first bout, hunger in the long middle of the third), `B39-L30-ANCHOR-07` — attachment to an object of no value (planned: the worn cord on her hilt, re-tied in Areas 04 and 06) and `B39-L30-ANCHOR-09` — taking an adult's offhand remark as literal law (registered; no Area is planned to render it) `[DERIVED]` (from: plan, the Open note on registrations and the Behavioral Plans of Areas 03 to 06). SIGNATURE (the planner's proposal, confirmed with the Chapter Spec on 2026-10-17): `B39-L08-REFLEX-03` — she reaches for the injured before the cause of the injury. Origin: `OPEN-AREA`.

**Bennet Kestle — Behavioral (Part 39, core-6):** L01 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 04; no plan item settles his body conduct beyond the held stance recorded at L29); L10 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 04; plan evidence on record: in the bout he waits for his opponent to finish a sentence before he answers it (Area 04 anchor 1), and he answers a child's question in one plain line (anchor 5); the nearest catalog options each add a clause the plan does not give, so no code is picked); L03 `OPEN-AREA` (Due by: Chapter 1.1.1.004 Area 04; no plan item settles how feeling reaches the surface); L08 `B39-L08-REFLEX-02` — reaches for the record; the first instinct is to establish what actually happened `[DERIVED]` `[SIGNATURE]` (from: plan, Area 04 anchor 4 and Behavioral Plan: after the bout he asks which touch came first and abides by the judge's call); L29 `B39-L29-WAIT-04` — takes a formal held position and maintains it exactly `[DERIVED]` (from: plan, Area 04 anchor 1 and Behavioral Plan, which names it as his core Layer L29: he waits at the rail in a held stance until the ring is clear); L34 origin — `OPEN-AREA` (the plan gives no origin for the Signature). AGE BAND: `B39-L30-BAND-02` — Adolescent (Part 16 YC-2) `[DERIVED]` (from: age fourteen, the author's figure; Part 16 Area 16.1, ages 12 to 16). ANCHORS (Rule 39.34.1.1): `B39-L30-ANCHOR-02` — attention captured by the wrong thing at an important moment (planned: at the call his eyes go to the loser's blade and not to the judge, in Area 04, and he watches the judge's hand and not the ring when the turn comes, in Area 05), `B39-L30-ANCHOR-03` — hunger, tiredness or cold arriving suddenly and completely, overriding everything (planned: tiredness arriving suddenly and completely at the break-up after the long morning, as he sits down on the end of a bench, in Area 06) and `B39-L30-ANCHOR-11` — sulking, and the specific unmistakable form of it (registered; no Area is planned to render it for him) `[DERIVED]` (from: plan, the Open note on registrations and the Behavioral Plans of Areas 04 to 06, as the confirmed Chapter Spec 1.1.1.004 stands after its light amendment of 2026-10-17; the anchor for a naive, unanswerable question is no longer in his set, because his question about which touch came first is his Signature at work and not a naive child's question). SIGNATURE (the planner's proposal, confirmed with the Chapter Spec on 2026-10-17): `B39-L08-REFLEX-02` — he reaches for the record: his first instinct is to establish what actually happened. Origin: `OPEN-AREA`.

**Chapter 004 page record, Gate 2 of 2026-10-18 (Ref: 1.1.1.004): Ansel Roke, Kerra Tonkin and Bennet Kestle.** Chapter 004 — The Measure of a Gift is the Chapter that shows Roke mended and the first Chapter that shows Kerra and Bennet. The registered lines above are unchanged and stay `[DERIVED]`; this paragraph holds what the page shows. No value is `[ASKED]`, no `OPEN-AREA` catalog code is picked at this Gate 2 (a Layer value is a registration step that the author reviews in a digest, Part 39 Rule 39.3.3.1), and no plan word is restated as a page fact. Check #21 at the Gate 1 of 2026-10-18: Roke SILENT; Kerra and Bennet decided by the Gate 1 checker (Tier C, Part 43) as `[KEEP PROFILE]`, reasons recorded (the Signature and the anchors render as registered and the page sets the genders the planner proposed); no Evolution Entry; no drift. The Pending rows for Kerra and Bennet are removed; what remains open for each is `OPEN-AREA`, not flagged.

**Ansel Roke (Chapter 004).** His Signature `B39-L08-REFLEX-10` fires a second time (the first was Chapter 003): at the bed he wakes "a guard's waking" and "his eyes counted the room before they reached anyone in it", and his first words after the thanks are a low question about the road (heard only as "...that the road was known..."). L01 `B39-L01-GAZE-05` and L07 `B39-L07-COUNT-01` show in the same count ("I counted twenty-one days of this house's hands, Madam"). His level is shown again as ***Warrior Level 1 Mid*** (the page's string and the primary healer's spoken diagnosis). Open lines, with the page evidence on record: L10 stays `OPEN-AREA`; he thanks her plainly and answers "Madam"; L03 stays `OPEN-AREA`; he is thin-voiced and plain and says what he owes in a count of days. Address forms: the healers call him "Captain Roke" (the author's ruling of 2026-10-17), Valia calls him "Ansel" and he answers "Madam"; what the mine's guards and carters call him, and what he calls them, is not engaged (the optional visiting guards do not appear) and stays `OPEN-AUTHOR`.

**Kerra Tonkin (Chapter 004).** Her level is shown as ***Mortal Tier 1 Blood Stage***, by her own salute ("I am Kerra Tonkin, Blood stage") and by Aegon's explanation to Petroc, before the first blow. Her Signature `B39-L08-REFLEX-03` fires: after the yield she drops her point and takes the heavier pupil's wrist in both hands before she does anything else. Registered lines the page shows: L30 `B39-L30-ANCHOR-03` (a tiredness that "had waited all through the bout" comes down into her shoulders as she sits, and hunger arrives all at once in the long bout) and `B39-L30-ANCHOR-07` (she re-ties the worn cord on her hilt while the second bout runs and finishes the knot through the Grandmaster's speech); `B39-L30-ANCHOR-09` is not rendered. Open lines, with the page evidence on record: L10 stays `OPEN-AREA`; she talks all through the bout in full sentences, taunt and banter, and she answers Varya's "Can I try?" with "Not yet" "as plainly as she would have given the time, with no apology in it and no scorn"; L29 stays `OPEN-AREA`; the cord is the only waiting beat the page shows; L01, L03 and the L34 origin stay `OPEN-AREA`. She uses ***Leafdash***, ***Leafhop***, ***Firm Glissade*** and, afterwards, ***Midrib Stay*** (Manual Ledger moves; no move block here, Rule 42.12.4.3). Gender: the page writes her she.

**Bennet Kestle (Chapter 004).** His level is shown as ***Senior Level 1 Mid*** by the salute, by Silas ("They are both Seniors of the first Level, in the middle of it") and by the string, before the first blow. His Signature `B39-L08-REFLEX-02` fires: after the bout his eyes go to the pale patch on Loveday Rowse's blade, and he asks the judge "which touch came first" and abides by her call. Registered lines the page shows: L29 `B39-L29-WAIT-04` (he holds his wooden sword upright before his chest at the rail until the rake and the pupil with it are off the sand); L30 `B39-L30-ANCHOR-02` (at the call his eyes go to the loser's blade and not to the judge, and in the long bout he misses the turn of the sword "watching the Grandmaster's hand") and `B39-L30-ANCHOR-03` (he sits down hard at the end of the near bench at the break-up and lets his head fall back); `B39-L30-ANCHOR-11` is not rendered for him. Open lines, with the page evidence on record: L10 stays `OPEN-AREA`; he waits for his opponent to finish speaking before he answers ("Bennet waited until she had finished, and then a moment longer") and answers a child's question in plain short lines; L01, L03 and the L34 origin stay `OPEN-AREA`. He uses ***Midribstrike***, ***Leaf Pace*** and ***Foiblelid*** (Manual Ledger moves; no move block here). Gender: the page writes him he.

**Bennet Kestle (Chapter 007, Ref: 1.1.1.007; Gate 2 of 2026-10-21).** He is glimpsed in the Eighth Hall in the Head Elder's round (Area block 01, in Kellan Rothe's viewpoint), speaks no line and is not spoken to. His level is shown again as ***Senior Level 1 Mid*** by a short identification in the narration ("a Senior of the first Level, middle of it"), the route Chapter Spec 1.1.1.007 set for him. His age-band anchor `B39-L30-ANCHOR-02` (attention captured by the wrong thing at an important moment) is rendered once: his eyes stay on his own careful steps and do not lift as the Head Elder passes. No Signature of his fires, no energy is read, and no registered line is contradicted; the page shows no change of his profile, so this Gate 2 writes no Evolution Entry and no drift for him. The registered lines are unchanged and stay `[DERIVED]`; nothing is set at `OPEN-AREA`.

**Chapter 009 page record, Gate 2 of 2026-10-23 (Ref: 1.1.1.009): Wenna Tarr, Halric Mear and Brya Tull.** Chapter 009 — The Pass Holds is the first Chapter that shows the three, all in Area block 05, in Corin Vale's viewpoint, on the morning of 1247.3.27 / PD 86: Wenna Tarr at the trough of Stonemill Crossing at first light, Halric Mear and Brya Tull at the muster stone just inside the Hammer-and-Hilt Gate. The registered lines of 2026-09-29 above are unchanged and stay `[DERIVED]` (one `[ASKED]` each, as written); this paragraph holds what the page shows. Check #21 at the Gate 1 of 2026-10-22: `[KEEP PROFILE]` for all three (Tier C, decided by the Gate 1 checker with the reasons recorded: the page shows each Signature and the registered core lines and no change of any line); no Evolution Entry; no drift. No value is `[ASKED]` at this Gate 2 and no `OPEN-AREA` catalog code is picked (a Layer value is a registration step that the author reviews in a digest, Part 39 Rule 39.3.3.1). **Wenna Tarr.** Unawakened ("There was no qi in her to read"). Her Signature `B39-L29-HANDS-02` fires (the hub pin worked in the wait, the next strap found by feel). Registered lines the page shows: L01 `B39-L01-HANDS-02` (the hands find a piece of the rig and work it, and do not stop); L10 `B39-L10-FILLER-03` (she asks after the road, the weather and the load); L03 `B39-L03-CHANNEL-07` (feeling into work on an object: she draws a strap one hole tighter than it needs). She is the Chapter's third ordinary mouth that names the makers, in passing: "It is the queen's wire, and Bramwell Cog's thimbles, and the Iron Dawn's smithing". She uses no vocative but "Captain". **Halric Mear.** "A Senior of the middle Levels." His Signature `B39-L10-NAME-06` fires (the name first in every address: "Corin Vale, Captain of the Wing, returning from the Twinpost", before anything else). Registered lines the page shows: L29 `B39-L29-HANDS-05` (his hands go to the roll) and L08 `B39-L08-LINE-01` (the record will say only what the runner says, which is the nearest the page comes to the line). **Brya Tull.** "A Senior of the lower Levels." Her Signature `B39-L01-STILL-02` is shown as the exception that reads: she, who "had never stood on both feet at once", stands on both feet. Registered line the page shows: L10 `B39-L10-FILLER-08` (she offers a seat and water). Address forms: she says "Captain"; no form is coined.

Qi: none; Hercules's sense finds nothing under her skin ("no qi at all"), and she is Unawakened, as nearly everyone in the village is. Mind, feeling and soul: brisk and dry with Hercules ("You are always slow with the rope"), laughing once, a bark, when he corrects the baker's count of loaves; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20:** Body: bitten in the forearm by a wolf in the bakery yard on 1247.2.15 / PD 43 (Area block 03); carried in at the bakery door by Mawgan Rosewarne with the arm wrapped to the elbow in the baker's apron, "white to the lips"; folded into a sling by the healer in the hall (Area block 05); on 1247.2.17 / PD 45 her left arm is in the sling, and toward evening the healer takes her wrist between two fingers and says it is "slower than yesterday and that was the right way for it to go" (Area block 06). Mind, feeling and soul: stands in the yard "not running" with a tray of the evening's loaves and asks Hercules, "brisk and dry as she had been at the well", whether the bell means the bread as well (Area block 02); cries out once "a short furious sound" when the wolf takes her arm and says through her teeth "Somebody pick up those loaves, because that was the hall's whole supper!" (Area block 03); asks "How many?" of the loaves and, told that thirty-one are fit to eat, says "Then the hall eats short, and it will not forgive me", barks one laugh at her own words and stops it with her teeth, keeping her eyes on Hercules's face and never letting them go lower (Area block 03); swears briefly at the healer over the sling (Area block 05); at the Hedge Well on Day 17 says nothing at all about the yard and tells him "You are still slow with the rope" "not unkindly", the smile coming "after her words instead of with them" (Area block 06). **Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), 2026-10-22:** Not on the page; spoken of by Bison on the high ground on 1247.2.21 / PD 49 (Area block 01). Body: "has her arm out of the sling and a basket on it" (the sling of 1247.2.17 / PD 45). Mind, feeling and soul: the baker "is still scolding her for the basket"; nothing recorded for her own part. |
| **Pasco Cobb** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 002 — The Well at First Light (Ref: 1.1.1.002) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-22 | Toll collector at Alder Bridge, who works the bar, counts each load and each person who goes through, and marks a slate that hangs from the post | ROLE-COLLECTOR, B39-L30-BAND-05, F3, PSM-1, PSM-7, B39-L07-COUNT-04, B39-L10-LISTEN-02, B39-L29-HANDS-05[SIG], RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI], CC-S1[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], OMI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: no wound recorded; sleeves rolled to the elbow as the morning sun warms the road. Qi: none; his count is all slate and habit, with no qi under it. Mind, feeling and soul: even and exact over the count, speaking his tally to the slate without looking up, counting Hercules with his yoke as he counted the cart and passing him by name and errand ("Hercules, that is two buckets for the bakery, so go through"); nobody asks what he has written; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20:** Body: whole; in his shirtsleeves, he runs up from the bridge "with nothing in his hands" (Area block 02). Mind, feeling and soul: counts aloud the people who run past him and carries Ebrel Tangye up the hall's slope on his back ("Nineteen steps to the door, and I will say every one of them aloud, so there is no need to look."); takes his place on the hall's threshold "as he did at the bridge bar" and counts the people through (Area block 02); at the end of the fight stands in the row at the hall door with "his mouth open, and no number coming out of it", having "stopped counting" (Area block 03); in the hall sits on an upturned bucket beside the Chief's table with the slate he had gone back for at the bridge post and the pencil on its string, and says "Every household in the lane is on this slate, and each has come in here, or been counted in the mill loft, or the inn cellar, or the forge cellar, so I make it that none is missing", drawing a stroke and then, "because his hand did it of itself", another (Area block 05); on Day 17 mends the bar of Alder Bridge with a new pole and a bolt, counting each blow of the mallet as it falls, gives the cost of the night from the slate in the mill square ("Nobody is lost, and I have counted it three times"), writes down the Chief's plan and puts Hercules and Bison down for the third night of the watch, "the same as any other pair" (Area block 06). **Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), 2026-10-22:** Not on the page; spoken of on the high ground on 1247.2.21 / PD 49 (Area block 01). Mind, feeling and soul: his slate "had put the two of them here tonight as a pair, like any pair" (the narration) and the watch is "as Pasco wrote it" (Hercules); Bison says the bar of Alder Bridge "has not stuck since Pasco mended it", and Hercules says that Pasco, passing, told the drover at noon "it had been a bad winter for thatch"; nothing recorded for his own part. |

| **Neot Hosken** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 002 — The Well at First Light (Ref: 1.1.1.002) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-17 | The carter's lad, who does not speak in the Chapter, coils the cart's slack rope to keep his hands busy and carries a bent iron spike at his belt | ROLE-CARTLAD, YC-2, F3, PSM-2, PSM-8, B39-L01-GAZE-09, B39-L10-FILLER-04[SIG], B39-L29-WAIT-05, B39-L30-ANCHOR-03, B39-L30-ANCHOR-07, B39-L30-ANCHOR-10, RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI], CC-S1[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], LBI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: unhurt; sat down in the mud of the lane with his hand still on the cart's rail and shivered though the day was warm. Qi: none; the fist that held the crane rope had "nothing in it yet but a boy's grip", and the yard around him is Unawakened to the last lad. Mind, feeling and soul: silent in both scenes. In the mill yard he takes hold of Bison's coat, lifts his eyes with an effort to the jammed crane rope and points, hands Bison the spike, takes it back with the free end of the crane rope closed into the same fist, and pulls; in the lane he runs beside the sliding cart with his mouth open on a shout that makes no sound, is blamed by name by the carter before the whole lane, is cleared by the wheelwright's finding, and leans a little at a time toward Hercules, the nearest warm thing; nothing recorded for soul. |

| **Enyd Carrow** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 002 — The Well at First Light (Ref: 1.1.1.002) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-20 | Basket-weaver of the lane, whose own basket lay in the ditch after the cart came down, and who bends a willow rod into a ring and straightens it again while she speaks | ROLE-BASKETWEAVER, B39-L30-BAND-05, F3, PSM-1, PSM-5, B39-L10-TURN-06, B39-L13-CONFLICT-07[SIG], B39-L29-HANDS-03, RLM-SENIOR-1-LOW[ASKED], CC-S2[ASKED], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], OMI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: no wound recorded; her basket lay in the ditch. Qi: a trace so faint that a candle flame at her elbow would not have stirred, ***Senior Level 1 Low***, the first rank of those who have any at all, found by Hercules's sense; she does not practise on the page, and when he watched her face for the second look, the hitch of someone who had felt more in him than a strong back, it did not come. Mind, feeling and soul: puts the lane's trouble into plain words while working a willow rod in her hands ("There was a child at the ditch and a loaded cart loose in the lane, and the hall will want to hear this."), looking at the ditch where her basket lay; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20:** Body: whole. Qi: ***Senior Level 1 Low*** again, found by Hercules's sense: "A trace of qi lay in her, so faint that a candle at her elbow would not have flickered ... as it had in the lane before"; she does not practise on the page (Area block 06). Mind, feeling and soul: comes up the lane on Day 17 with baskets on both arms and a willow rod in her hand, "bending it into a ring and straightening it as she walked", and says "I told the Chief before anyone could argue that the stores should go in willow and not in sacks, and these are the first thirty, and the rest are still green on the rod"; Hercules watches her hands bend the rod and carries the baskets in (Area block 06). |

Mind, feeling and soul: slow and certain: silent for the length of three breaths with his thumb in the bright place, then reads the worn axle end aloud to the wheel and tells the lane "This is no lad's doing"; on the third morning the cart sits in his yard on two wooden props with its near wheel off; nothing recorded for soul. |
| **Ebrel Tangye** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 002 — The Well at First Light (Ref: 1.1.1.002) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-20 | A child at the lip of the ditch by the lane, who holds a pebble up to the sun; she does not speak in the Chapter | ROLE-CHILD, YC-1, F3, PSM-2, PSM-4, B39-L01-OBJ-06[SIG], B39-L27-FEAR-07, B39-L29-WAIT-10, B39-L30-ANCHOR-02, B39-L30-ANCHOR-07, B39-L30-ANCHOR-10, RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI], CC-S1[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], LBI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: unhurt (nobody was hurt); stood against Hercules's leg with the pebble in her fist, then was given to the woman who came running for her. Qi: none; she has no trace of qi in her, only a small fast heart, and whatever told her where the safest place in the lane was, it was not a sense like Hercules's. Mind, feeling and soul: did not move for the whole of the cart's slide, and moved only when it was over, straight to Hercules; silent; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20:** Body: unhurt (frightened and never hurt). Mind, feeling and soul: her mother does not let go of her hand when a wolf has its jaws in the mother's shoulder; she comes away from her mother's chest "with a pebble shut in her fist" and stands "with every part of her stopped" before she goes up Pasco Cobb's back "as if it were a ladder she had been waiting for" (Area block 02); in the hall she crosses the floor "without a sound" to Bison, stands against his leg with the wool of his coat in one fist and a pebble in the other and does not look up, her hand "cold and dry and small" and clinging "as a hand clings to a rope" (Area block 05); she speaks no word in the Chapter, and on Day 17 her mother says she "has not said a word since, which is no change, but she has not let go of that pebble" (Area block 06). Her age-band anchors (`B39-L30-ANCHOR-02`, `-07` and `-10`) are carried from her row and are not re-picked. |

Mind, feeling and soul: steady and silent at the sowing, looking at the shut buds along the hedge and not at the sun; his first words of the morning are about Bison ("That one likes the view, and you will find him up there looking at it most days"); nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: a trace, ***Senior Level 1 Low***, the same faint first rank as the basket-weaver's, found by Hercules's sense; the quiet of his boots owes nothing to it (that is a forester's craft), and he does not practise on the page. Mind, feeling and soul: unhurried and exact; lets Hercules look at the marked trunk and the unmarked one before he speaks, then says which comes down in autumn and which stays; keeps to the gap in the hedge as if the field were a room he had not been asked into; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20:** Body: whole. Qi: ***Senior Level 1 Low*** again, "the same faint first rank as before", which "owed nothing to the quiet of his boots" (Area block 06). Mind, feeling and soul: on Day 17 stands at the edge of the circle in the mill square "no farther in than the gap in a hedge, as if the square were a room he had not been asked into", waits until the talk has gone round and come to rest, and says "The forest went quiet before it came. I was at the far corner at midday, and I heard it go, the small birds first and then the squirrels and then the..."; he stops, and the pause is long enough that three people open their mouths and think better of it, and he ends "After that there was nothing at all, as if the whole edge had been put under a cloth" (Area block 06). |

A hook drew him off his seat at the first blow and left him on his back beside the near wheel with his hand moving on the ruts; the driver who lay beside that wheel "in the path of the edge" did not move again when the front three swept the road, and the Captain's count finds "a driver" on his back at the lead wheel. Gareth Sefton names him ("Edric Gower drove the lead wagon and was talking to his horses when they took him") and Crowe reports him among the dead at the Court. Qi: unawakened; Roke's sense "found nothing in them, only the blankness of bodies that had never taken qi in". Mind, feeling and soul: easy and talkative with his team to the end ("Whoa now, my beauties, steady. It is only a fox in the road, and we will be in the yard before full dark."); nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: unawakened (Roke's sense finds "only the blankness of bodies that had never taken qi in"). Mind, feeling and soul: defiant for his team and his wagon and not for himself ("Get off my wagon, you thieves! There is nothing on it for the likes of you, and I have a team to see to!"), while the clerk begs him by name to let the raiders have the chests; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), 1247.1.2 / PD 2, afternoon:** in the third of the eight beds of the ward of Aster House, three weeks after the raid, with his right shoulder "bound so thickly that he looked to have grown a second, white one"; he lifts his good hand as Valia passes and she says "Hobb" without stopping; she tells the ward that she is walking past him and Emmet because the man at the window end is the worst hurt and "the house goes to the worst first, whoever is standing in the door", and that she will come back and look at each of them properly; on her way out she stops at his bed (and at Emmet's) as she said she would. |
| **Emmet Wicker** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 003 — The Lone Banner (Ref: 1.1.1.003) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-17 | Driver of the fourth wagon of the Bluecleft shard caravan, who calls for water from under his wagon when the raid is over | ROLE-DRIVER, B39-L30-BAND-05, F3, PSM-4, PSM-8, B39-L03-CHANNEL-02[SIG], B39-L10-FILLER-06, B39-L29-SILENCE-01, RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI], CC-S1[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], PCI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: alive and in need of help; a thin voice from under the fourth wagon asks for water, the Captain sends Hasler's pair "to make stretchers and to carry water to the man calling under the fourth wagon", and Gareth Sefton names him ("Emmet Wicker, who drove that team"); the page names no wound and does not say what is wrong with him. Qi: unawakened (Roke's sense finds "only the blankness of bodies that had never taken qi in"). Mind, feeling and soul: calls out in a thin voice ("Please... is anyone there? I would give anything for a drink of water."); the Captain heard him and "did not go. The gap came first."; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), 1247.1.2 / PD 2, afternoon:** in a bed of the ward of Aster House (the page does not describe him or his hurt), three weeks after the raid; Valia names him with Hobb as she walks past ("Hobb, Emmet, I am walking past you, and I want you both to know why") and stops at his bed on her way out, as she said she would. |
| **Perrin Lisle** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 003 — The Lone Banner (Ref: 1.1.1.003) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Dead | 2026-10-17 | Clerk of the Bluecleft shard caravan, who sits on the second wagon with the book pressed against his coat and begs the raiders to take the chests | ROLE-CLERK, B39-L30-BAND-05, F2, PSM-1, PSM-8, B39-L01-HANDS-05, B39-L03-MASK-08[SIG], B39-L08-REFLEX-09, RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI], CC-S1[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], PCI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-2[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: dead; hauled over the far rail by a line fighter's ***Levering Hookpull*** and beaten ("the blows that followed were short and flat"), he lay across the ruts "with both arms over his face, breathing", was carried first to the patrol's post, and "died in the hospital in the night", the night before the Court's morning, Year 1247, Ilovany, Day 2 (Crowe's report at the Court). Qi: unawakened. Mind, feeling and soul: afraid and pleading ("I am only the clerk, and the book is in order, and the seals are whole, so take them, take whatever you came for! I have a boy at home... I have never done anyone any harm."); "the healers say he talked of a boy at home"; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), 1247.1.2 / PD 2, afternoon:** the fourth bed of the ward of Aster House stands made up and empty, "its blanket folded back in a square and its pillow without a dent" (the page does not say whose it was); Valia's step does not change at it, but her free hand opens and closes once at her side; a few lines on, the primary healer tells her "the clerk went in the night, and he talked of a boy at home". |
| **Davin Tarn** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 003 — The Lone Banner (Ref: 1.1.1.003) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Dead | 2026-10-17 | Best spear of the Bluecleft mine guard, whose boot heel taps a small steady knock as he walks | ROLE-GUARD, B39-L30-BAND-05, F3, PSM-3, PSM-5, B39-L01-STILL-02[SIG], B39-L08-LINE-05, B39-L29-BEFORE-01, RLM-SENIOR-8-MID[AI], CC-S2[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], LBI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: dead; killed in the first rush on the lower road, Year 1246, Ulenor, Day 8: a halberd's axe edge "took him at the shoulder before he could turn from the wheel", and he "went down on the ditch side with the spear still in his hand and his heel quiet"; Gareth Sefton names him ("The one by the lead team is Davin Tarn") and Crowe reports him among the dead at the Court. Body: unhurt. Qi: unawakened (the intake is "forty-one children who had not woken to qi"). Mind, feeling and soul: curious and unhurried; he has "asked seven questions since the benches filled", the last of them why the sand in the grooves was white when the earth was brown, and "was losing the eighth to a beetle walking the arm of the bench"; he asks whether the cord of Aegon's *First Grip* is for the sword or for Aegon, takes Aegon's "Because the sword is on my hand" as "a whole answer" and goes back to his beetle, and asks "Why do they hit each other if they like each other?" when Aegon explains the Blood stage; he says that one of the older pupils took fifteen steps and Silas corrects him ("Fourteen"); nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the front classroom of Hall 5 he kneels on his bench with his back to the board and puts one question after another to an older unawakened girl behind him ("Do you keep it in your hands? Does it ever get hot? Is that why you wear the grey?"), stands with a question in place of an answer ("Is it by wanting to hold it, Teacher? Or is it, no, is it by being given some?") and is told to sit and make an answer out of what he has been told, which he makes at last out of her own words ("Nobody knows how a body comes to hold qi. You said so three times, Teacher, and I only heard it the third time"); he asks whether a person is strong "first, and the reason comes afterward, if it comes", asks Ned whether the king counts, says of his eyebrows, when Max Pryce tells him he is breathing with them, that "they wanted to see what was happening", asks Max what the clear wash on the sill is and why it has no smell; at the rack his point goes wide and finds the post, and in the afternoon he tells the bench, as something his mother says every time a barrow goes by, that the queen thought of the cord, the Grand Artificer's people stitched it and the Iron Dawn cut the leather. |

Qi: unawakened. Mind, feeling and soul: practical and a step ahead of the others on the first morning; she tears a strip from the hem of her sleeve with her teeth, winds it twice around the leather above her fist, ties it off and turns the sword against the bench to test it ("it stayed put"), tells Varya "The man at the rack says there is a narrower one coming on the second cart, but that crate is still nailed shut", and folds the leftover scrap into her fist and keeps it there; the Grandmaster names her among the five smallest children ("the *First Grip* is bound to your fist already, and this year you will learn what it is for") and her fist closes over the scrap "as if somebody might ask for it back"; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the front classroom of Hall 5 she laughs at Mabyn's pocket behind her left hand, "the right one closed in her lap as it had been closed for six weeks"; she tells her closed fist that Silas "has never had a fourth thing in his life", says of the question whether the king can count "The king can do a great many things, but I do not think he can do that", and gives her breath as "My ribs open one at a time, like the rungs of a ladder going up"; Max Pryce calls hers "the best breath on this bench, low and slow with not a shoulder moved", done "with one fist shut". At the rack she is next after Varya for the narrow covers (yellow cord), unties the strip of her own sleeve from the red cover with her teeth, looks at it, and closes her fist over it together with the scrap she had kept there. |

Mind, feeling and soul: tells the bench what his cousin says: to watch Kerra Tonkin, Bennet Kestle and Max Pryce, that "Max Pryce is the best, so nobody will fight him one against one and he always goes last", and that the big ones answer "not yet" to anything you ask to try "and that it is not a no"; asked which time "not yet" means, he says "He did not say, and I did not think to ask him"; the Grandmaster names him among the five smallest children ("you will learn to take ten breaths in one place without moving, and the tenth will cost you more than the first nine"), and he goes so still that the whole bench notices, and then the stillness gets into his knee; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the front classroom of Hall 5 he rocks "the same distance each time" and passes on what his cousin says (the ones at the back never talk to the intake; Hesta Cardew gives every intake its first lesson, has never been late and has never been loud); asked how many intakes that is, he says "He did not count them. He only said it the way you say the sun comes up." He goes still for the first time that morning at the exact answer; in the breath drill the stillness gets into his knee at the sixth of the ten breaths and shakes the bench, and afterwards he rubs the knee with both hands. At the rack his knee "had not yet forgiven the morning", he rocks heel to toe between calls and does the three moves "like a boy crossing ice", and he holds out his fist for the narrow cover "stiffly and a little late". |

At the rack she stops between calls to watch the nearest ring, which Jago Keverne lets her do, and she puts on her narrow cover "while she was still looking at the ring". |

I will be as tall as it by the end of the week."); the Grandmaster names her among the five smallest children ("your feet will find the first stance before the sword has grown to you, and the sword will wait for them"), and she stands the sword up in front of her, measures herself against it with the flat of her hand and nods at the result; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the front classroom of Hall 5 she comes in late with Tamar Hendra, "explaining that the passage was longer on this side"; she asks "Teacher, is it a pocket? Below the navel, I mean. A thing that is kept somewhere is usually kept in something", and is told "Nobody has found a pocket, Mabyn Tippett, or a wall, or a lid, and you have asked about the place where I stopped, so I am glad it was you"; she says a strong person is "for lifting" and tells of a carter and a cask in the street of the coopers; she nods at the exact answer "as if somebody had told her the size of a sword"; she gives her breath as "My nose is cold on the way in and warm on the way out, and the cold part lasts a little longer", and Max Pryce lifts her elbow with two fingers and sets it lower without her blinking. At the rack her feet find the first stance before her sword, which "wavered above her head like a flag on a short pole"; Jago Keverne stands beside her and does the stance himself, tells her "Your feet are right, Mabyn, and the sword is late. It will come when it is sent for", and she sends for the sword at once with two crooked fingers; with the narrow cover on, the sword "came to her chest and stayed". |

Qi: unawakened. Mind, feeling and soul: counts the older pupils' steps aloud ("the man at the rack had told him to learn the yard by heart"), corrects Petroc without turning his head ("Fourteen"), and tells the bench the floor's customs: the youngest sit on the benches, the Grandmaster's hand goes up when a bout is over and then it is over, the custom lets someone who wants to try the best come in numbers if they say so to the judge first, and in the long bout the one who comes in is "whoever has rested longest", after "three changes" of rest "and then they are in for two"; he reads the pupils' levels aloud ("They are both Seniors of the first Level, in the middle of it"); he is not among the five smallest children; nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), 1247.2.21 / PD 49:** in the front classroom of Hall 5 he counts the children in by name under his breath and gives the room the total "as if it were a ruling" ("Thirty-nine of us are in, and two are still in the passage"); he is first to say aloud that the ladder on the board makes eleven steps, and Varya, who had it at the same moment, is annoyed that he said it; asked what a strong person is for, he lists three things (strength, carrying a sword, defending a house) and "no fourth", and tells Brea he had several and did not say them; in the breath drill he gives "At the hold my heart is loud in my ears, and it was not the same loudness each time", counts Petroc's eyebrows (nine movements), and reads Max Pryce's level aloud to the room ("For a Senior of the third Level, high in it, you scrape a great many hands"). At the rack his point "went exactly where it should", and he says "The Iron Dawn cuts iron" to keep a thing in its place. |

| **Sigrun Korth** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 005 — The House With No Walls (Ref: 1.1.1.005) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Dead | 2026-10-19 | Captain of the Windward Host riding the Whistledeep mine escort in person, who stops every file on the shale ledge above Stirrup Ford, reads the morning first and recites the standing orders aloud at every halt | ROLE-ESCORT, B39-L30-BAND-05, F2, PSM-5, PSM-9, B39-L02-FIRST-01, B39-L15-ORDER-05[SIG], B39-L25-GUARD-08, RLM-WARRIOR-7-HIGH[ASKED], CC-S2[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], PCI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-1 |  | Body: dead on 1247.1.9 / PD 9 at Stirrup Ford. She holds the gravel bar with the water at her back against the two leaders and the line until her breath is short and a cold ache gets into her knees as a weight comes out of the trees; Rask puts the hook of his halberd behind her knee and "The edge came down once" (Area block 02); the lieutenant of Ridgewatch finds "the Captain and her five" on the gravel and in the water on the eleventh and names them from the Host's roll (Area block 05). Qi: ***Warrior Level 7 High***, the page's own string in her thought ("more than the Seam Road had ever asked of a captain"); Rask orders against "a captain who is high in the seventh Level". Mind, feeling and soul: she stops every file at the shale ledge and reads the morning first, recites the standing orders aloud at the halt and sets her five by name and Level, herself last ("Gant, take the lead down"); not easy about the road, she gives a man in a good brown coat walking away at the edge of the mist one look and no more, and keeps to what she said ("a file does not turn back on a feeling"); she sounds her horn once and nothing answers; her company's standing orders "went into the water with her" (the lieutenant's count); nothing recorded for soul. |

When the company lets its qi go his knees strike the gravel at the lead wheel, "and his palm was still on the hub"; he turns the second blow on the inside of his blade (***Tight Basket***, ***Swellbarrier***), and a cleave with qi in its edge sweeps the road ahead of him clean and "he went down under it" (***Cinderheave***, Area block 01); named among the dead (Area block 05). Qi: "of the fourth Level, high in it" (the captain's recital; the realm token in the code, Warrior Level 4 High, is the author's value). Mind, feeling and soul: comes to the captain from the wheels, "his palm left the last hub only when his stride took it", and reports "They are all cool, Captain, and nothing is running dry."; walks the lead wheel down into the ford with his palm on the hub; nothing recorded for soul. |

At the strongbox wagon's near wheel she turns a cut at her hand with her half-drawn blade slid against its scabbard (***Ready Backedge***, Area block 01) and a cut on the outside of her blade with a roll of the wrist (***Falsedge Barrier***, taken to be hers: "A woman at the near wheel", Area block 02); she is one of the three at the strongbox wagon pressed to the ground by the Cast stroke and finished by the centre rank; named among the dead (Area block 05). Qi: "fourth Level and the middle of it" (the captain's recital). Mind, feeling and soul: crouches at the lip of the ledge and traces the cutting, the bar and the far bank in the shale dust with one finger, and speaks only when the tracing is done ("The bar has crept east with the thaw, so the wagons go straight along it, and the willows on the far side are soft ground for anyone who has to run."); nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: "fourth Level and low in it" (the captain's recital). Mind, feeling and soul: stays where he is at the halt, "because Jorund Ulm never left the strongbox bench", so that the captain faces the second wagon and speaks to him with the rest; the strongbox behind him holds "the cargo list and the route papers", "the only paper on the road"; nothing recorded for soul. |

Among the plunging teams she braces her arm and takes a blow close to the hilt with the bones locked (***Resting Belly***, Area block 01) and brings her blade over her head in a full circle and down on a shoulder (***Whirlflog***, taken to be hers: "A woman among the plunging mules", Area block 02); she is one of the three at the strongbox wagon pressed to the ground by the Cast stroke and finished by the centre rank; named among the dead (Area block 05). Qi: "third Level and the top of it" (the captain's recital). Mind, feeling and soul: comes to the halt "with a mule's name still in her mouth" and talks to the mules without a pause ("Easy, grey. It is only water, and you have crossed it more often than I have."); in the mist her voice goes on "in one flat, steady stream"; her last words, said "to nobody", are "Easy, grey, easy, it is only water, and I am here."; nothing recorded for soul. |

Mind, feeling and soul: at his place beside the clerks' table; sees Stavik at the edge of the boards first, and his eyes go to the stiff black sleeve and back to the day's list; says each of the nine Gale names after Stavik, "in a flat voice, with his eyes on the planks", as two pens set each down; named by Stavik, with Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke, as a hearer of the count ("before Ranulf Skeld, and Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke of the Stone Vigil, who have heard it"); nothing recorded for soul. **Earlier, as of Chapter 005:** Body: whole; on his feet since noon (the wind finds "the heat of a man who had stood since noon", Area block 03). Qi: unawakened: the king's wind goes through him "without catching, since there was no qi in him to read". Mind, feeling and soul: crosses the flagstones to the king "with his hands folded and his eyes on his own feet, the walk of a man who has told two people no and expects to be overruled"; says that the Court has risen and that the last two "will not go down the Crossing until they have said it to your face"; the king asks him to walk behind him and look disapproving, and sends him to walk Hallgerd Vorn's stair in the morning and to send to the corridor post and ask Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry what they know; on the morning of Mavil he looks at the sky "with great care" when Knut Dahl says that Haug's father once complained of a wet corner, and turns his face to the Ring while the king reads Varys's letter; he says the Court's rising after the king, "naming the day", and is named as a hearer of the king's answer to Varys; at the queen's hand on his sleeve he nods and sends a boy running to see that the lieutenant eats and sleeps (Area block 06); nothing recorded for soul. |

Mind, feeling and soul: his terrace wall is down across his field and his neighbour Knut Dahl "said it had fallen of its own weakness"; he tells the king all of it ("The wall stood forty years, Your Majesty, until the drain was dug above it. I do not say Knut did it from spite, only that he never asked what stood under his drain."), his voice sinking as voices do near the king; he is set to come to the Court on the morning of Mavil with Dahl and afterward to walk up and look at the wall with him; his grey dog is on its feet before the king stops walking, goes round behind his legs and will not lie down, and his hand drops to its neck without a word; on the morning of Mavil he waits at the edge of the boards with the dog close against his leg, studies the planks while the king reads Varys's letter, and hears the king set the case aside ("The wall will wait the length of a letter, Ottar, and so will the drain."); at the Court's rising the petitioners go down the Wind Crossing and his dog goes first; the case is not settled; nothing recorded for soul. |

Mind, feeling and soul: nine of the thirteen petitioners of that day "wanted the same stair mended" because of it; nothing recorded for her own part. |

Mind, feeling and soul: waits her turn at the edge of the boards with her helmet under her arm and gives "a count of what I found, and it orders nothing" in full connected sentences: the four wagons standing in the ford with the sacks gone and the strongbox open and empty of its papers, the Captain and her five on the gravel and in the water, the four carters and the clerk under and beside the wagons ("All eleven are dead, and no one is left who saw it"); then the ground (about two dozen men, the butt-marks of long hafts, a train of pack-mules lost on the shale, strips of sacking torn off in a single pull, a strip of plain undyed cloth, one plain brown coat "of a kind sold at any market in the kingdom", blood that was not Gale's and none of the raiders' dead left behind), and that the Captain's company is without its standing orders; she says what the ground gives and what it does not give ("a name or a mark or a kingdom"); the king thanks her by name; she answers the queen's one question ("On the gravel bar between the strongbox wagon and the third, Your Majesty, with the water at her back.") and, led off to eat and sleep, looks back once at the queen "with the face of a woman who had been given a bed and not a word"; nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: ***Senior Level 6 Mid***, the page's own string ("the strongest qi in the village if Hercules and Bison were not counted", Area block 01); a pair of Warriors' pressure bends his knees "with not a tooth laid on him yet" (Area block 02). Qi: ***Senior Level 3 Low***, the page's own string, found by Hercules's sense at the hall door (Area block 01). Mind, feeling and soul: strikes the bell "hard and fast, by someone who knew exactly how hard a bell could be struck" (Area block 01); turns the village in at the door "as a woman turns a flock through a gate" ("Go in, all of you, and along the wall. Count your own as you come through, children first and then the old and then your neighbours, and give the number to Pasco at the door. Do not stop in the doorway to tell me what you saw, because it will keep"); says she has not seen Mawgan or Lowen come in and has no one to send, so Hercules should go if he can (Area block 02); in the hall thanks the two ("a village does not forget a debt of that size"), lets the quiet stand, and rules "What was seen tonight was seen by this village, and it stays in this village. It does not go out to the road, or to the inn's guests, or to any rider who asks for it", calling no vote (Area block 05); on Day 17 says the hedge was torn from the forest's side and that she would hear nobody say whose gap it was, because it was nobody's, and proposes a watch at the forest's edge two at a time from dusk to the grey, the hedge mended better than it was, stores for the hall (grain, salt and dried meat) and a bell on a post at the head of the slide, and the village agrees (Area block 06); nothing recorded for soul. The page's own forms for her are "the Village Chief" and "the Chief". |

Qi: none; unawakened (Area block 02, where the lanes are read as "all of them unawakened"). Mind, feeling and soul: looks at the two hands that still hold the beam "longer than a man should" with his mouth open on a question; says "Slag and ashes. Four of us hung that lintel, and I cut the joint myself, and I will say this for the bear: the joint did not give" (Area block 02); in the hall crosses the floor with one of the thirty-one loaves in both hands and says he stood under that lintel a day and a half hanging it, "and tonight I lay under it with a wolf on my knee, and I have been working at how a man lifts it ever since, and I cannot make it come out right. So I have brought you bread", and sits down on the floor with the loaf when Hercules says the joint was cut true: "It was a good joint" (Area block 05); on Day 17 hangs a new door in the hole the bear left with Hercules and Bison at its two ends, his eyes on his own hands and the joint he cuts with a chisel no wider than his finger, and when it is true lays his palm flat on it and leaves it there (Area block 06); nothing recorded for soul. |

Mind, feeling and soul: screams once, "broke off in the middle of a note", and stands under the left leaf of the inn's stable doors, which "hung from its top hinge alone", "with one arm up as if the door were a dog she could tell to stay", while a wolf comes along the wall toward her; when Hercules has turned the falling leaf over the wolf she says, before he has finished asking whether she is hurt, "That hinge has been loose since the thaw. I told the lad twice, and the third time I told myself, which is the one I did not hear" (Area block 02); in the hall, from the bench by the hearth, asks Bison, who came off the oven shed onto the bear "as if it were a stair", to say something, "because the whole room wants to" (Area block 05); on Day 17 stands by the stable doors of her inn and tells whoever holds the new hinge which way it goes before he has finished turning it over, says it was "not the hedge, it was whatever came through it", and calls the Chief's plan "a good plan" (Area block 06); nothing recorded for soul. **Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), 2026-10-22:** Not on the page; spoken of by Bison and Hercules on the high ground on 1247.2.21 / PD 49 (Area block 01). Mind, feeling and soul: that afternoon she asked Bison to look at her cellar flap, "looking at my face", held the lamp for him and "stood nearer than she needed to", and when he went put a pie in his hands (mutton, hot, and she told him it was left over); at noon she told a drover who asked what had burned at the edge of the village that it was "last winter's thatch" (Hercules: "I watched the room for a second look at either of us, and it did not come"); nothing recorded for her own part. |

I have kept this bakery all my life, and I never knew a fox to leave bread alone", looks at the loaves, at Hercules's back and at the bear, says nothing more and goes in (Area block 03); on Day 17 stands in the bakery's back door in his flour and steps aside without a word when Hercules brings the new frame (Area block 06); nothing recorded for soul. |

| **Hesta Cardew** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 007 — A Day on the Floor (Ref: 1.1.1.007) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-21 | Older teacher of the unawakened in the front classroom of Hall 5 of Sapphire Ascent Academy, a Senior of the eighth Level who has stood at it nineteen years, who sets the benches to a string and teaches the stages and the realms from a board | ROLE-INSTRUCTOR, B39-L30-BAND-06, F3, PSM-3, PSM-5, B39-L01-HANDS-11, B39-L06-MEM-02, B39-L10-TURN-07, B39-L16-CORRECT-03, B39-L29-WAIT-04[SIG], RLM-SENIOR-8-MID[ASKED], CC-S2[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], TRI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: whole; no wound or mark is shown. She kneels at the window end of the front classroom in the grey before the first bell with a string drawn taut along the front of the benches, one end pinned under her knee and the other wound round her fist, and corrects each bench with her heel; at the board her broad hands are "grey with old chalk in the creases" and she wipes her fingers on a cloth (Chapter 007, Ref: 1.1.1.007, Area blocks 01 to 03). Qi: ***Senior Level 8 Mid***, in her own words ("I am a Senior of the eighth Level, in the middle of it, and I have stood in that place for nineteen years"); the page says none of it reached the benches, and the children cannot read her. Mind, feeling and soul: exact, unhurried and not cross. She says "Elder Rothe" to the Head Elder without looking up and lets his reply go by without an answer. In the lesson she stands with her hands behind her back, lets the room fill and talk, and begins from Tamar Hendra's question of six weeks; she says aloud the three places where her knowing stops (where qi comes from, why a body is able to hold it, what keeps it where it is put) and will not fill them "with a guess"; she draws a door, seven stages and four realm names as a stair of eleven steps, sees a crooked line and leaves it, says she gives four realm names "because I teach only what I have met", lets a little banter run and brings the room back with a question or a plain word, and calls each child by name. She gives her own view of what a strong person is for (someone others can lean on without having to ask), answers Varya's question with "Nobody can say the day" and a reason in three parts, names the First Awakening and gives no age, season, count or sign, and closes with "Keep asking them"; she hands the room to Ben Morris and goes out by the side door without looking back; nothing recorded for soul. |
| **Ben Morris** | Side Character — Tier D (Part 19) | Chapter 007 — A Day on the Floor (Ref: 1.1.1.007) | none — this entry is the entity's whole ledger record | Active | 2026-10-21 | Younger teacher of the breath in the front classroom of Hall 5, a Senior of the fifth Level with ink on one cuff, who counts the unawakened through four in, four held and six out, then ten breaths in one place | ROLE-INSTRUCTOR, B39-L30-BAND-05, F3, PSM-1, PSM-7, B39-L06-INFO-10, B39-L10-TURN-01[SIG], B39-L29-WATCH-01, RLM-SENIOR-5-MID[ASKED], CC-S2[AI], AFN-NONE[AI], SOUL-NONE[AI], HEART-NONE[AI], PCI[AI], IS-1[AI], PIS-1[AI], CPT-5 |  | Body: whole; no wound or mark is shown. A young man with ink on one cuff who comes in at the main door as the older teacher goes out by the side one, with his hands loose at his sides, and then stands where she stood (Chapter 007, Ref: 1.1.1.007, Area blocks 03 and 04). Qi: ***Senior Level 5 Mid***, in his own words ("I am a Senior of the fifth Level, in the middle of it, so what I do with my breath is not what you will do with yours. Mine carries something. Yours carries only air, and that is enough to learn on"); Aegon cannot feel it. Mind, feeling and soul: calm and kind; his voice does not rise at the ends of its sentences, and he waits until the room has let its breath go before he goes on. He teaches the breath in three parts (a hand on the belly and a hand on the chest to find which moves first; then four in, four held and six out for five rounds with no word from anyone, himself included; then ten breaths in one place), asks each child to say in their own words what they notice, calls what the children feel "the body talking", tells Aegon "Then you have the knack of it already", lets the room talk between counts ("Talk, if you like. The breath is only something you do, and you may put it down"), and leaves Max Pryce to walk the benches; as the second bell rings he is saying something kind about the morning; nothing recorded for soul. |

He stands before the three ranks of the intake with his arms folded, "of the Soul stage, the last of the body's seven, and it showed in nothing but the ease with which he stood"; he does the step of ***Leafdash*** beside Aegon with no sword in his hand, "at an easy pace", and the first stance beside Mabyn, and he fits the five narrow covers himself by closing the leather round each fist and drawing the cord across the back of the hand (Chapter 007, Ref: 1.1.1.007, Area block 05). Qi: none of his own; a man of the Soul stage of Mortal Tier 1, in the page's words ("of the Soul stage, the last of the body's seven"); no level string is written for him. Mind, feeling and soul: calls ***Midrib Stay***, ***Leafdash*** and ***Leafhop*** "in a voice that carried to the far ring without being raised", so that the children "leaned on it as they might lean on a wall"; he corrects by doing the movement beside the pupil and says nothing of the fault; he tells Mabyn "Your feet are right, Mabyn, and the sword is late. It will come when it is sent for", says "Clean your hands" when the last call is done, lets Tamar watch the nearest ring, and says of the narrow covers "These are the narrow ones. Varya will have hers first, since she has waited longest, and the rest of you will follow in the order you stand"; nothing recorded for soul. |

She stands at the second gate of the Library's stair with a book shut against her chest and moves it to her other arm (Chapter 007, Ref: 1.1.1.007, Area block 01). Qi: none; unawakened, "one of the forty-one unawakened children who had come in together", this year's intake. Mind, feeling and soul: the Head Elder finds her first of all the readers because she is "the one furthest behind the rest"; the keeper on the stool "looked at her, and at the rope, and said nothing, and she did not ask"; she goes on waiting; she speaks no line; nothing recorded for soul. The page gives no reason for the gate and no rule that opens it. |
