Qi: a Warrior, one of the soldiers the page gives as "Warriors all, of the fourth Level and above"; no string is written for him. Mind, feeling and soul: goes red to the ears when Corin names the boot and explains at once ("It was only the stream below the second shelter, Captain. I went through it for the water, and the pot needed the water."); goes for the pack "with his shoulders up"; crawls three arms' lengths saying the names of those he passes into the stone ("Reuben Gask," "Marden Sewell"); on the road says that he has been four months on the Pass and never heard of raiders, and that he saw red cloth under a torn coat ("I saw it by the coals, Captain, the same as you") and "did not like to be the one" to say so; Corin leaves him at the Accord Hall to eat with his boots off until sent for; nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: a Warrior (the page's "Warriors all, of the fourth Level and above"); no string is written for him. Mind, feeling and soul: dry and quick at the table ("I will say this for Gale cooking, that it makes a little meat go the whole length of a table"); puts a hand to his chest "as if run through" when Corin answers with the count in the book; says "you were gone, and I could not turn my head to see where"; is told to take Reuben Gask to the left end and close the gap; draws in a breath to speak of the red under a torn coat and lets it out through his nose; goes down the south road with Haldor Vinje and a torch and comes back with the evening pair; keeps his hand under Reuben Gask's head as he dies; on the road asks how a man can be hit and say nothing, and what to say if the Grand Marshal asks what it means ("you will tell him you do not know"); is left at the Accord Hall to say what he saw to the Grand Marshal and to nobody before him; nothing recorded for soul. |

Qi: a Warrior (the page's "Warriors all, of the fourth Level and above"); no string is written for him. Mind, feeling and soul: "did not look up from the bone he was working at" while the table teases, and answers it dryly ("a man needs a second pair to carry the first"); named by Stavik with Corin Vale and Marta Kell as a witness of the watch order; told "Haldor, bring the ones who are hurt, and do not stop for a blade", reports that he has the hurt behind the shelters and that he could not get to Brita Thune "because the shed is on her", is told to say the rest when the stone is at his back, and says nothing more; goes down the south road with Marden Sewell and a torch; receives Stavik's standing orders in the open, low and even and for a long while, with Corin Vale, Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry named as witnesses, and answers "I have heard them, Commander, and I keep them", which is all he says; Stavik calls him "the senior man of the Gale half" at the Aerie (Area block 08). He decides nothing on the page; the author ruled at the Gate 1 of 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 that he stays Tier D; nothing recorded for soul. |

You may enter the quiet in your book if you like. I had it in my head that you were a sergeant."); sent across the open stone into the wire house with Marta Kell by Corin's voice during the fight (Area block 02); a witness to Stavik's handover and to Corin's message; stays at the post with the six hurt; he speaks no other line on the page; nothing recorded for soul. |

Men who are running do not stop for their dead, and these stopped for every one they could lift. Nobody shouted, either. I kept waiting for somebody to shout, and the tall one only walked, and I kept waiting for him to say something."); asks later whether the Court will say the names of the dead out loud ("Will the Court say our names, Commander?" and "Will they be said out loud, where people hear?") and is told "All nine, Finn. The steward says them after me, and the clerks copy the count out for the families"; nothing recorded for soul. |

**The ninety-second to ninety-eighth rows, Colm Ardley, Marden Sewell, Haldor Vinje, Marta Kell, Ewan Mabry, Sten Marrick and Finn Hallgren, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 009 (Ref: 1.1.1.009), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-23, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7; the Gate 1 checker returned all seven as passing as submitted) and written token for token as the hand-off passed them; this Gate 2 proved each conduct token against the Part 39 catalog by search (every option text exists, none is `99`, `PENDING` or `[DEPRECATED]`), the ascending Layers with one conduct code from any Layer, and the single `[SIG]` mark of each code, and found no break (the other tests are the Gate 1 checker's). **Names.** The Chapter Spec 1.1.1.009 names no Tier D person (Part 38 Rule 38.2.2a.1): Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry were named in speech in Chapter 005 (Ref: 1.1.1.005) and had no row until they appeared, and the author accepted both as unawakened and living on 2026-10-22; the other five names are the Area Maker's own picks. The Gate 1 name scan, and this Gate 2's whole-word, case-insensitive scan over `story-memory` (outside the session log, the archive and the specs) and over the written Chapters, found no earlier carrier of any of the five names. Name notes (information, not reopened; the Gate 1 checker read them as notes and the author did not rule otherwise): Marden and Marta share a stem and stand in one scene (Area blocks 01 and 04), Haldor Vinje and Orm Haldane share the Gale "Hald-" stem on one page (Area block 02), and Colm echoes Colan Rundle (Chapter 004). Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Who they are.** Colm Ardley, Marden Sewell and Haldor Vinje are soldiers of the detachment's two halves at the post below the Twinpost (Area blocks 01 to 05); Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry are the post-master and her clerk of the wire house (Area blocks 01, 02 and 04, and spoken of in Area block 06); Sten Marrick and Finn Hallgren are two of the four riders of Stavik's escort to Highgale (Area block 07). **Haldor Vinje.** He receives Stavik's standing orders over (Area block 04), which the Gate 1 checker flagged under Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4; the author ruled on 2026-10-22 (the Step 0) and again on 2026-10-23 (the Gate 1) that he stays Tier D, a promotion to Tier C being open only if he recurs. He decides nothing on the page. **The nine named dead are not here.** By the author's ruling of 2026-10-23 (the Gate 1 question on the named dead) the nine named Tier D dead are recorded on the Chapter's casualty roll and in the Wing's ledger casualty record (`ledger-people-society-faction/northern-accord-expeditionary-wing.md`), and take no Tier D row each: Davit Mercer and Ketil Rann (the evening pair), Tavis Ansley, Reuben Gask, Aslak Rime, Brita Thune, Orm Haldane, Signe Vaag and Leidulf Stang. Six more Azure Sky dead and three more Gale dead are not named on the page. **Status.** All seven are Active. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade, office or kind word and is not always a page word: the page writes "post-master" and "her clerk" once each, in Corin's report (Area block 06), "the key" in Marta Kell's speech and "escort" once, of Stavik's four riders, and it writes none of "guard" or "operator" as a role of anyone (the ROLE-GUARD and ROLE-OPERATOR of the codes); it shows Marta Kell by her ink-stained hand, her key and her wire house, and Ewan Mabry by his supper read as though it might need his signature. The age-band tokens (YC-3, BAND-05) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives "the boy" for Colm Ardley, "the youngest of them" for Finn Hallgren and no age for the other five. **Realm tokens.** The Warrior tokens carry `[AI]`; the page gives the rank and file only as "Warriors all, of the fourth Level and above" (Area block 01) and the riders as "four Warriors of his escort", so the Level number inside each token is the Maker's reading and no string is written on the page for any of the five; `RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI]` for Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry agrees with the page ("no qi in either to read") and with the author's acceptance of 2026-10-22, so the mark may be raised to `[ASKED]` on the author's word; no token was changed here, because only the author alters a token (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.6 item 5). The stratum tokens stay `[AI]` because they are derived from the realm. **Gender.** The page writes Marta Kell as she, and Colm Ardley, Marden Sewell, Haldor Vinje, Ewan Mabry, Sten Marrick and Finn Hallgren as he. **Pair-Senior.** Blank for all seven: no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the seven speaks or does the ordinary work of a post or a ride, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4); the Gate 1 checker recorded no Tier D consequence flag for the seven beyond the question on Haldor Vinje above. Check #21 for the seven: SILENT (Tier D, decided by the Gate 1 checker; no change of any code is shown). **Existing rows used in the Chapter.** Ranulf Skeld, Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke (Area block 08) are existing Tier D rows, and their Current Condition cells gained a Chapter 009 line; Wenna Tarr, Halric Mear and Brya Tull (Area block 05) are Tier C and Tamsin Holt (Area block 06) is a Tier A character with her own file, and are recorded in their own places. Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the unnamed dead (six of Azure Sky and three of Gale), the other two of Corin Vale's four on the road (the man with the bound hand and the man with the cut scalp), the other two riders of Stavik's escort, the six hurt who stay at the post and the two runners, the Accord Hall's clerk, the clerks at the Court, the carters and the crowd at the Crossing and the Gate, and the company of the raiders.

**The eighty-seventh to ninety-first rows, Hesta Cardew, Ben Morris, Jago Keverne, Zeb Olver and Eseld Quintrell, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 007 (Ref: 1.1.1.007), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-21, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7) and written token for token as the hand-off passed them. The Maker's hand-off records that its first set of codes was lost in a context break and that these were re-picked from the catalogs; this Gate 2 read each conduct token of the five codes against the Part 39 catalogs (V2: every option number exists and is not `99`, `PENDING` or `[DEPRECATED]`), the segment order, the ascending Layers with at most one conduct code from any Layer, the one `[SIG]` mark of each code and the three `L30-ANCHOR` codes of the two children, and found no break; the other tests are the Gate 1 checker's. **Names.** Hesta Cardew and Ben Morris were set by the author on 2026-10-20 (the planner proposed Hesta Cardew at the author's request and the author confirmed her; the author chose Ben Morris in place of the planner's first proposal for the younger teacher, which the Gate 1 checker found to echo Leofwin Pellam); Jago Keverne, Zeb Olver and Eseld Quintrell are the author's names, given on 2026-10-21 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.007, Settled and Names). The author's rulings keep all five Tier D, an exception to the note on Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.1 that a person a Chapter Spec names is not Tier D, recorded in the Audit notes of that spec and covering these five persons and no others; each takes a row at this one Gate 2 with a code picked at the writing. The name scans recorded in that spec (2026-10-20 and 2026-10-21, outside the archive, the agent memory, the temporary folder and git) found no hit for any of the five names. Name notes (information, not reopened): the page writes the younger teacher as "Ben Morris" in full every time and never as the bare "Ben"; Bennet Kestle (Area block 01 only) and Ben Morris (Area blocks 03 and 04 only) share no scene; the near names Kellan and Kestle stand beside Keverne, and Jago Keverne (Area block 05 only) shares no scene with Kellan Rothe or Bennet Kestle. Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Who they are.** Hesta Cardew and Ben Morris are the two teachers of the unawakened in the front classroom of Hall 5 of Sapphire Ascent Academy (Area blocks 01 to 04): she gives the first lesson (the stages, the realms, what a strong person is for, and why no one can say the day) and he the second (the breath). Jago Keverne is the man at the rack of Chapter 004, whom that Chapter's page left unnamed and the author named on 2026-10-21; he calls the afternoon drill and fits the fourth width of the *First Grip* (Area block 05). Zeb Olver is the boy at the lowest nail of the Quest Hall's board and Eseld Quintrell the girl at the Library's second gate, both met in the Head Elder's round (Area block 01). **Status.** All five are Active. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade, office or kind word and is not always a page word: the page writes "teacher" of the two named teachers, "the man who handed the swords out and called the drill" of Jago Keverne, "a pupil of the Tendon stage" of Zeb Olver and "one of the forty-one unawakened children" of Eseld Quintrell, and it does not write "rackkeeper". The age-band tokens (BAND-06, BAND-05, BAND-05, YC-2 and YC-1) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives no age for any of the five (Hesta Cardew says only that she has stood at her Level nineteen years, and Ben Morris is "a young man"). **Realm tokens.** The two teachers' tokens carry `[ASKED]` as the author's own levels (***Senior Level 8 Mid*** and ***Senior Level 5 Mid***, Chapter Spec 1.1.1.007, Settled); `RLM-SOUL[ASKED]` for Jago Keverne and `RLM-UNAWAKENED[ASKED]` for Eseld Quintrell are the author's rulings of 2026-10-21 (that Jago Keverne holds the Soul stage of Mortal Tier 1 and that Eseld Quintrell is unawakened, one of the intake of forty-one); `RLM-TENDON[AI]` for Zeb Olver is the Area Maker's pick and agrees with the page ("a pupil of the Tendon stage"). The stratum tokens stay `[AI]` because they are derived from the realm. **Gender.** The page writes Hesta Cardew and Eseld Quintrell as she, and Ben Morris, Jago Keverne and Zeb Olver as he. **Pair-Senior.** Blank for all five: no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the five speaks or does the ordinary work of a post or a place in a queue, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). Zeb Olver stands before a nail from which, behind the Head Elder, a slip comes off, the one act of the five, which carries no consequence beyond the Head Elder's reading of it; the author's naming of him as a Tier D person is the ruling that Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.6 item 2 asks for a newcomer who acts, and the Gate 1 checker recorded no Tier D consequence flag. Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the Library's keeper and the keepers on the stools at the rope gates, the early readers, the Quest Hall's clerk, the monitors in brown coats, the Lower Treasure Hall's counter-keeper, the small pupil with the coin, the instructors at the post of the Sixth Hall and at the far end of the Seventh, the pupils of the Sixth to the Ninth Halls other than Colan Rundle and Bennet Kestle, the Academy's older unawakened pupils on the back benches (among them a tall girl at the end of the last bench), the Iron Dawn's barrow-hand, and the palace's lamp-lighter (a taper seen far off, no person shown).

**Chapter 007 lines on the rows already held (Ref: 1.1.1.007; Gate 2 of 2026-10-21).** The Current Condition cells of Petroc Curnow, Brea Lawry, Ned Bolitho, Tamar Hendra, Mabyn Tippett, Silas Uren and Colan Rundle gained a Chapter 007 line each, and Bennet Kestle's and Max Pryce's cells and Kellan Rothe's file were updated; no Code cell was touched (the Code cell is Baseline, Part 37 Area 2.11). The page of Chapter 007 writes Tamar Hendra once with a pronoun ("Tamar first with her eyes on the floor", Area block 02), which sets the pronoun she; Chapter 004 had left it open. The six intake children are on the benches of the front classroom of Hall 5 (Area blocks 02 to 04) and in the three ranks at the rack (Area block 05) and speak in whole sentences throughout; Silas Uren counts the room in as thirty-nine with two still in the passage, which agrees with the intake of forty-one. No Tier D person of the earlier Chapters was promoted or changed in kind.

**The seventy-second to eighty-sixth rows, Selyf Chenoweth, Yestin Hocking, Austol Bawden, Tegen Angwin, Morwenna Keast, Tomas Truscott, Jenifer Pender, Jory Michell, Mawgan Rosewarne, Gwenna Tangye, Kerensa Kneebone, Alys Spargo, Mylor Retallack, Delen Gluyas and Sawle Hambly, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-20, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7) and written token for token as validated and as the hand-off passed them after the Gate 1 fix loop (the four codes of Alys Spargo, Mylor Retallack, Delen Gluyas and Sawle Hambly were compared character for character with the validated strings at the last Gate 1 pass). The first eleven names, Selyf Chenoweth, Yestin Hocking, Austol Bawden, Tegen Angwin, Morwenna Keast, Tomas Truscott, Jenifer Pender, Jory Michell, Mawgan Rosewarne, Gwenna Tangye and Kerensa Kneebone, were given or accepted by the author on 2026-10-19 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.006, Settled), three of them the author's own renames of the same day (Mawgan Rosewarne, Jory Michell and Tomas Truscott), and the Chapter Spec plans each by trade, level and age; the author ruled the persons who act or decide Tier D with no promotion (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). The other four names are free names made in the fix loop of the Gate 1 audit of 2026-10-20 (Check #17(a), Major, Part 28 Rule 20.2 prong 1, and Check #20 element 20-1: four quoted speakers had no name and no Tier D row): Alys Spargo, Mylor Retallack, Delen Gluyas and Sawle Hambly stand in the text the author confirmed at `[CONFIRM WRITE]` on 2026-10-20 and are MINOR persons; no separate author consultation for them is recorded on disk (Delen Gluyas replaced the first pick, Veryan Gluyas, in the same loop, because Veryan echoed the hero Varya). Each first name and surname was scanned as a whole word, case-insensitive, over `story-memory` outside the session log and the archive, and over the written Chapters, before this Gate 2: no hit beyond the Chapter's own records and the one earlier Tangye (Ebrel Tangye of Chapter 002, who is Gwenna Tangye's daughter). Name notes (information, not reopened): Mylor Retallack and Mawgan Rosewarne share the initials M.R. in one Chapter; Jory Michell shares its first three letters with Jorund Ulm (Chapter 005). Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Realm tokens.** The four Senior tokens (Yestin Hocking `RLM-SENIOR-6-MID`, Selyf Chenoweth `RLM-SENIOR-4-MID`, Morwenna Keast `RLM-SENIOR-3-LOW`, Jory Michell `RLM-SENIOR-2-LOW`) carry `[AI]` as validated, and each agrees with the page's own string (***Senior Level 6 Mid***, ***Senior Level 4 Mid***, ***Senior Level 3 Low***, ***Senior Level 2 Low***); the Chapter Spec records the four levels as values the author accepted with the names (Settled, 2026-10-19), and Chapter 005 marked an author-given realm `[ASKED]`, so the mark may be raised to `[ASKED]` on the author's word; no token was changed here, because only the author alters a token (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.6 item 5). The unawakened tokens agree with the page ("no trace of qi", "unawakened", "nothing at all"). **Who they are.** Selyf Chenoweth, Yestin Hocking, Austol Bawden and Tegen Angwin are met at the head of the log slide at dusk on 1247.2.15 / PD 43 (Area block 01); Morwenna Keast is heard at the hall's bell there and seen in Area blocks 02, 05 and 06; Tomas Truscott, Jenifer Pender, Jory Michell, Mawgan Rosewarne and Gwenna Tangye are met in the lanes in Area block 02; Alys Spargo is at a shutter beside the bakery yard (Area block 03); Mylor Retallack is on a doorstep along the torn lane and Delen Gluyas in the mill loft hatch (Area block 04); Sawle Hambly and Kerensa Kneebone are in the hall (Area block 05). **Status.** All fifteen are Active and none dies. Yestin Hocking (thigh and forearm) and Gwenna Tangye (shoulder) are bitten, and Tomas Truscott is pinned under the forge door's beam; all three are tended and live. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade, office or kind word and is not always a page word: the page writes "swineherd", "smith", "mill men" and "a mill man", "baker", "weaver", "carter", "healer" and "thatcher", and it writes none of "woodcutter", "hunter", "keeper", "innkeeper", "foreman" and "spinner" (the ROLE-WOODCUTTER, ROLE-HUNTER, ROLE-KEEPER, ROLE-INNKEEPER, ROLE-FOREMAN and ROLE-SPINNER of the codes); it shows Selyf Chenoweth by his crew and the logs, Austol Bawden as an old man who had cut timber on the slope all his life, Yestin Hocking by his bow and his walk of the edge, Morwenna Keast by the bell, the hall's door and the title the Village Chief, Jenifer Pender by "her inn", Jory Michell by the nine mill men and the mill gate, and Gwenna Tangye as Ebrel Tangye's mother. The age-band tokens (BAND-05, BAND-06, BAND-07 and YC-2) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives an age or a band for three only: Tegen Angwin is "fourteen" (YC-2), Morwenna Keast "a broad woman of sixty or so" and Austol Bawden "an old man". **Gender.** The page writes Selyf Chenoweth, Yestin Hocking, Austol Bawden, Tomas Truscott, Jory Michell, Mawgan Rosewarne, Mylor Retallack, Delen Gluyas and Sawle Hambly as he, and Tegen Angwin, Morwenna Keast, Jenifer Pender, Gwenna Tangye, Kerensa Kneebone and Alys Spargo as she. **Pair-Senior.** Blank for all fifteen: no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the fifteen speaks or does the ordinary work of a trade or a post, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4); the acts the Chapter Spec settled for the Tier D persons (Yestin Hocking's hold, Gwenna Tangye's shield, Pasco Cobb's carrying of Ebrel Tangye, Mawgan Rosewarne's drag, the Chief's ruling, the two crews at the gate) are settled as no promotion, and the Gate 1 checker recorded no Tier D consequence flag. Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the five men of Selyf Chenoweth's crew and the nine mill men (each by head only), the lad on the bell rope, the woman who tells Mylor Retallack to hush, the villagers at the shutters and the loft's other faces, the workers on the strips and the file of men at the far ford.

**The fifty-sixth to seventy-first rows, Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott, Egil Hurn, Ranulf Skeld, Ottar Haug, Hallgerd Vorn, Ivar Lund, Knut Dahl, Ragna Holm, Sverre Vik, Dagmar Sund, Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 005 (Ref: 1.1.1.005), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-19, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7) and written token for token as validated and as the hand-off passed them after the Gate 1 fix loop. The seven names and levels of the escort's captain, the five of her escort and the lieutenant (Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott, Egil Hurn and Dagmar Sund) are the author's own, ruled Tier D on 2026-10-18 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.005, Settled), so the realm token of each of the seven carries `[ASKED]` in place of `[AI]`; the stratum token of each stays `[AI]` because it is derived from the realm. The other nine names are free names made in the build of the Chapter (Part 28 Area 20.3): Ranulf Skeld, Hallgerd Vorn, Ivar Lund, Ragna Holm and Sverre Vik were picked in the drafting pass, and Ottar Haug, Knut Dahl, Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke in the fix loop of the Gate 1 (Ottar Haug replaces "Eskil Haug" of the first draft, renamed so that no first initial pairs with the author's Egil Hurn, the code unchanged; Knut Dahl was named after the Gate 1 found the farmer's neighbour speaking and recurring unnamed, and Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke likewise for the Stone Vigil's two, Check #17(a), Major, Part 28 Rule 20.2); the four are the drafting pass's picks and not the author's, MINOR persons, with no author consultation owed (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.005, variance note G4 of the hand-off). Each first name and surname was scanned as a whole word, case-insensitive, over `story-memory`, the skills, the guardrails, the commands, the agents and the written Chapters before this Gate 2: no hit. Name notes (information, not reopened): Skeld, Sund and Sverre are three S names in one scene (Area block 05); Torkel (Hallgerd Vorn's grandson, named in speech only) echoes the stem of the author's Torvek. Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Who they are.** Sigrun Korth is the Captain of the Windward Host who rides the Whistledeep mine escort in person; Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott and Egil Hurn are the five of her escort; with the four carters and the clerk (no rows) they die at Stirrup Ford on the morning of 1247.1.9 / PD 9, killed by the company that Wulfric Rask leads. Ranulf Skeld is the Court's steward at the Aerie Palace; Ottar Haug, Knut Dahl and Hallgerd Vorn are unawakened petitioners (Haug and Vorn at dusk on 1247.1.12 / PD 12; Dahl and Haug at the Court on the morning of 1247.1.14 / PD 14); Ivar Lund and Sverre Vik are the two glider pilots of the Windward Host's Flightcraft arm who bring Aegon's letter and Varys's letter; Ragna Holm keeps the third station of the ward-line; Dagmar Sund is the Lieutenant of the Windward Host's Ridgewatch detachment who gives the count of the raid; Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke are the two of the Stone Vigil named at the Court. **Status.** Six are Dead (the escort): the lieutenant names the Captain and her five among "all eleven" dead in her count (Area block 05), and the page shows Korth's end and the end of the escort's line in Area block 02; the other ten are Active. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade, office or kind word and is not always a page word: the page writes "captain", "her five", "escort", "steward", "petitioners", "pilot", "keeper", "Lieutenant", "Airman" and "Corporal" and, for Nyland and Bakke, "stood watch for the Stone Vigil"; it writes none of "usher", "farmer", "sentry" and "rider" (the ROLE-USHER, ROLE-FARMER, ROLE-SENTRY and ROLE-RIDER of the codes). The age-band tokens (BAND-05, BAND-06, BAND-07) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives no age for any of the sixteen except "old" for Hallgerd Vorn, "of middle years" for Ottar Haug, "young" for Sverre Vik and "stout" for Knut Dahl. The realm tokens agree with the page's level strings (***Warrior Level 7 High*** and ***Senior Level 9 High***; the escort's recital; "a Senior of the lower Levels" for Lund and "another Senior of the lower Levels" for Vik; "a Senior of the middle Levels" for Nyland; "a Senior of the upper Levels" for Bakke); the stages in the tokens of Lund, Vik, Nyland and Bakke are the Maker's reading; the unawakened tokens agree with the page ("found no qi", "no qi in him to read", "a stout unawakened man", "an unawakened woman"). **Gender.** The page writes Sigrun Korth, Yrsa Wold, Ingrith Mott, Hallgerd Vorn, Ragna Holm and Dagmar Sund as she, and Torvek Gant, Jorund Ulm, Egil Hurn, Ranulf Skeld, Ottar Haug, Ivar Lund, Knut Dahl and Sverre Vik as he; **Gudrun Nyland's and Leif Bakke's gender is not written on the page** (the narration says "their faces to the plateau" and uses no pronoun for either), so it stays `OPEN-AREA` and no pronoun is canon. **Pair-Senior.** Blank for all sixteen: no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the sixteen speaks or does the ordinary work of a post, a petition or an escort, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4); the deaths of the escort and of the five hands were put to the author under that Rule and ruled as planned (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.005, Settled). Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the four carters and the clerk of the caravan, the unnamed second leader and the line fighters of the company, the clerks of the Court, the groom of the corridor post, the boys sent running and at the queen's door, the man in the good brown coat and the rest of the Stone Vigil. Named in speech or in a letter and not present, so given no row here (Part 37 Threshold C-1): Torkel, Marta Kell and Ewan Mabry; the pupils Brea and Kerra in Aegon's letter and Davin Tarn, Wil Harker, Edric Gower, Perrin Lisle and Ansel Roke in Varys's letter keep the rows and entries they already hold.

**The first seven rows above are pre-registered as plan baseline** for the Ninth's first appearance (Soldier of the Ninth, pair-seniors, picked codes). They are confirmed or amended at Gate 2 when first shown to the author. Whether any of the seven holds a rank title is not established. **The eighth row, Ivo Lathram, is not plan baseline:** it was written at the Gate 2 of Area 02 (Ref: 1.1.1.001.02), the Area of his first appearance, from the code validated at Gate 1 and confirmed at `[CONFIRM WRITE]` on 2026-10-06. He is the palace's night wire clerk, named at the build of Chapter Spec 1.1.1.001 (Round 1). He speaks and does his ordinary service; no choice and no story-state act of his is shown, and any consequential act goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). His Pair-Senior cell is blank because no formation registers such a position for him. The squad count and the pair-senior ruling live at `ledger-people-society-faction/azure-sky-military.md` §3 and §6.7 and `ledger-people-society-faction/northern-accord-expeditionary-wing.md` §6.1, cited and not restated.

**The ninth to twelfth rows, Nessa Pryor, Garth Maddock, Ruan Fenner and Orla Maddock, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of Area 04 (Ref: 1.1.1.001.04), the Area of their first appearance, with the codes the Area Maker picked, exactly as the orchestrating command passed them. The Gate 1 audit of 2026-10-06 validated the codes of Nessa Pryor and Garth Maddock against the first draft; the text written is a later plain-English rewrite that was not re-audited, and the codes of Ruan Fenner and Orla Maddock were picked and checked against V1 to V9 by the Area Maker and were not audited at Gate 1 (a light read at this Gate 2 found no break of V1 to V9; the codes were not changed). Nessa Pryor and Garth Maddock were named at the build of Chapter Spec 1.1.1.001 (Round 1). Ruan Fenner and Orla were named at drafting in Garth Maddock's speech; the registry name Orla Maddock takes her husband's surname, which the text does not write. Ruan Fenner and Orla Maddock are spoken of and are not on the page, and the rows were written as the orchestrating command directed, though a name only mentioned would not by itself meet the threshold (Part 37 Threshold C-1). No consequential act of any of the four is shown (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). Garth Maddock's trade word, "miller", is fixed for him at this first use (A16.4a in `ledger-people-society-faction/azure-sky-kingdom-state.md`). Their Pair-Senior cells are blank because no formation registers such a position for them.

**The thirteenth to fifteenth rows, Lowen Wray, Pasco Cobb and Kitto Hale, were kept through the redraft of Chapter 002 and are written from the whole-built Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002); they are not plan baseline.** They were first registered at the Gates 2 of the by-area build of that Chapter. The Areas of that build were archived at Step 2 of `/redraft-chapter` on 2026-10-07 and are not canon from then on. By the author's ruling of 2026-10-07 the three people stand: each row kept its name, its kind (Side Character, Tier D) and its picked code, and its first-appearance cell, its one-line descriptor and its Current Condition read `pending redraft` until the one Gate 2 of `/create-chapter` for Chapter 002, which wrote them on 2026-10-08 from the written text. The three codes are the picks of the archived build; the Gate 1 of the whole build closed with 0 FAIL and no code was changed. Chapter Spec 1.1.1.002 plans the three by trade (the baker's helper, the toll collector, the cooper), and the ROLE token of each code carries the trade. The text writes the toll collector's trade ("the toll collector, Pasco Cobb") and shows the other two at their work (dough dried on Lowen Wray's cuffs and the bakery door she works at; Kitto Hale's leather apron gone black from years of wet oak and a full cask), but it writes neither the word "baker's helper" nor the word "cooper". The age-band tokens of the three codes (YC-3, BAND-05, BAND-06) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives no age for any of them. Their Pair-Senior cells are blank because no formation registers such a position for them.

**The sixteenth row, Talan Yeo, is kept through the redraft and is not used in the whole-built Chapter 002.** The author's ruling of 2026-10-07 keeps the row (name, kind and picked code), and Chapter Spec 1.1.1.002 plans him by trade (the one who looks after the stables of Rowantree Inn); the written Chapter does not show him, so his first-appearance cell, his one-line descriptor and his Current Condition still read `pending redraft`, and the row stays as it is until a Chapter shows him or the author rules otherwise. His Pair-Senior cell is blank because no formation registers such a position for him.

**The seventeenth to twenty-fifth rows, Jowan Angove, Neot Hosken, Senara Polglase, Cador Roskilly, Enyd Carrow, Newlyn Pengelly, Ebrel Tangye, Alun Tredinnick and Uthno Nancarrow, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-08, with the codes the Area Maker picked, exactly as the orchestrating command passed them from the hand-off file of that Chapter; the Gate 1 of the whole build closed with 0 FAIL. The nine names were set by the author at Step 0 of `/create-chapter` on 2026-10-07 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.002, Settled). Neot Hosken and Ebrel Tangye do not speak in the Chapter. The text writes "the carter", "the carter's lad", "the basket-weaver", "the wheelwright" and "a forester" and writes "the child" and "the girl" for Ebrel Tangye; it shows Senara Polglase at the scale, Cador Roskilly coming out of the grist mill and Alun Tredinnick at his strip without writing the words "sack-weigher", "miller" or "farmer", which come from the Chapter Spec and the ROLE token of each code. The age-band tokens (BAND-05, BAND-06, YC-1 and YC-2) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives no age for any of the nine. Each of the nine speaks or does the ordinary work of a trade, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). Their Pair-Senior cells are blank because no formation registers such a position for them.

**The twenty-sixth to thirty-ninth rows, Edric Gower, Hobb Marlow, Emmet Wicker, Perrin Lisle, Davin Tarn, Jessa Lowe, Gareth Sefton, Wil Harker, Hasler, Ferris, Hew Larkin, Merrin Oakes, Amos Hartley and Piers Coyle, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 003 (Ref: 1.1.1.003), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-17, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7) and written token for token as validated; the Gate 1 of the whole build closed on 2026-10-17. Chapter Spec 1.1.1.003 names no Tier D person (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.1); the fourteen names are free names made in the build of the Chapter (Part 28 Area 20.3), and the names of Gareth Sefton and Emmet Wicker stand as the author approved them on 2026-10-17. Name notes from the Gate 1 scan (information, not reopened): Gareth Sefton echoes Garth Maddock (Chapter 001); Merrin Oakes echoes Maren Cole; Hobb Marlow echoes Selene Marrow (and the Part 3 stage word Marrow); Edric Gower echoes Edran Voss; Emmet Wicker echoes Wick Culver, a plan-baseline row not yet on the page; Wil Harker and Emmet Wicker rhyme on "-ker". Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Who they are.** Edric Gower, Hobb Marlow, Emmet Wicker and Perrin Lisle are the caravan's carters and clerk, who do not fight and are unawakened (the page reads them as absence: "only the blankness of bodies that had never taken qi in"); Davin Tarn, Jessa Lowe, Gareth Sefton and Wil Harker are guards of the Bluecleft mine, Seniors; Hasler and Ferris are soldiers of Captain Tregear's patrol, Warriors; Hew Larkin, Merrin Oakes, Amos Hartley and Piers Coyle are met in the Signet Court's morning. **Status.** Four of the fourteen are dead by the close of the Chapter: Edric Gower, Davin Tarn and Wil Harker on the lower road at dusk, Year 1246, Ulenor, Day 8, and Perrin Lisle in the hospital in the night before the Court's morning, Year 1247, Ilovany, Day 2. Their Status cells read Dead, the status established in prose (Part 37 Area 2.11 gives the Status cell as "Active — or the status established in prose"); the other ten read Active. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade or office word and is not always a page word: the page writes "driver", "clerk", "guard", "soldiers" and "pairs", "keeper of the hay market gate" and "runner", and it writes neither "yard worker" for Hew Larkin and Merrin Oakes (the page shows them only by name and by what they do) nor "patrolman" for Hasler and Ferris. The age-band tokens (BAND-05, BAND-06, YC-3) are the Area Maker's reading; the page gives no age for any of the fourteen except "a young man" for Hew Larkin. Jessa Lowe's page level is "a Senior of the sixth Level", and the stage High of her realm token is the code's own; every other level string of the fourteen is the page's own, and the unawakened tokens agree with the page. **Pair-Senior.** Hasler is the senior hand of the first pair and Ferris the senior hand of the third (the formation registers a pair-senior: `ledger-people-society-faction/azure-sky-military.md` §3 and §6.7); the page shows only that Hasler "ran at the front with the horn" and that Ferris "led the third pair". Their pair-mates are unnamed and have no row. The other twelve Pair-Senior cells are blank because no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the fourteen speaks or does the ordinary work of a trade or a post, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4). Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the rest of the patrol (six soldiers "between the third Level and the fourth"), the other carters, the guards who kept the banner at the rear, the boy from the toll-house, the porter and the others of the queue at the yard door, the rider, and the orderlies.

**The fortieth to fifty-fifth rows, Berta Thaxter, Ida Norcott, Rafe Ingram, Petroc Curnow, Brea Lawry, Ned Bolitho, Tamar Hendra, Mabyn Tippett, Silas Uren, Colan Rundle, Loveday Rowse, Dewi Moyle, Wynne Opie, Jenkin Lanyon, Eira Sleeman and Arthen Carne, are not plan baseline.** They were written at the Gate 2 of the whole-built Chapter 004 (Ref: 1.1.1.004), the Chapter of their first appearance, on 2026-10-18, with the codes the Area Maker picked alone from the catalog text, validated at the Gate 1 by tests V1 to V9 (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.7) and written token for token as validated and as the Gate 1 report passed them. Chapter Spec 1.1.1.004 names no Tier D person (the thirteen working names of the pupils and the bench peers are held in the build-plan file, outside the spec, as Chapter Spec 003 held its own; Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.1 and Part 38 Rule 38.2.2a.1 read differently, and the author kept roles in the spec). The thirteen names were confirmed by the author on 2026-10-17, with three swaps on the Gate 1 checker's advice (Lewin became Kenver and then Petroc; Idris became Arthen). The names Berta Thaxter, Ida Norcott and Rafe Ingram were made at the Gate 1 fix loop of 2026-10-18: the Gate 1 checker found three speaking or acting persons without a name (the primary healer, the young healer and the Signet Court runner; Check #17(a), Major, Part 28 Rule 20.2 prong 1), and the author ruled "Name all 3 and register as Tier D" (2026-10-18); the Settled address note ("the unnamed primary healer 'Healer'") is read with the name, since the children say "Healer" and the name where the page gives one. This Gate 2 scanned the three new names as whole words, case-insensitive, over the repository outside the archive, the agent memory, the temporary files and the session log: no hit but the Chapter itself. Name notes from the Gate 1 scan (information, not reopened): Arthen Carne beside Enyd Carrow (Chapter 002); the K-sound cluster Kerra, Colan, Kestle and Kellan; Ned beside Neot, Tamar beside Tamsin, Dewi beside Davin, Colan beside Cole, Uren beside Ruan, Rowse beside Roke and Rothe and Lawry beside Lowe (the last seven kept by the author on 2026-10-17). Originality against published names is not cleared and is flagged for information. **Who they are.** Berta Thaxter and Ida Norcott are healers of Aster House (a primary healer at the Champion realm and a young Warrior); Rafe Ingram is the Signet Court's runner for the queen's side of the palace (not the Sword Court's runner, Piers Coyle); Petroc Curnow, Brea Lawry, Ned Bolitho, Tamar Hendra, Mabyn Tippett and Silas Uren are six children of the unawakened intake ("forty-one children who had not woken to qi"; the page gives no age, the plan reads them as eight, and the codes carry the YC-1 band), of whom Brea, Ned, Tamar and Mabyn are named by the Grandmaster among the five smallest children (with Varya, who is the fifth by the plan; the page names the four and then addresses Varya); Colan Rundle and Loveday Rowse are the losing pupils of the first and second bouts, and Dewi Moyle, Wynne Opie, Jenkin Lanyon, Eira Sleeman and Arthen Carne are the five of the long bout (the page gives no age, the plan reads them as thirteen to fifteen, and the codes carry the YC-2 band). **Status.** All sixteen are Active; no hurt on any of them passes a bruise, and no child under twelve is harmed. **Page and code.** The ROLE token of a code is a trade, office or kind word and is not always a page word: the page writes "healer", "runner", "pupil" and "the five", and shows the caller of the five only by what Dewi Moyle does ("I call the changes"). The age-band tokens (BAND-05, YC-3, YC-1 and YC-2) are the Area Maker's reading, and the page gives no age for any of the sixteen. The realm tokens of the pupils agree with the page's level strings (Colan ***Mortal Tier 1 Blood Stage***, Loveday ***Senior Level 1 Mid***, the five ***Senior Level 2 High***); Berta Thaxter's token agrees with her page string (***Champion Level 8 Mid***); the stage of Ida Norcott's token (Warrior Level 2 Mid) is the Maker's reading of the page's "a Warrior less than a year"; the unawakened tokens of the children are the codes' reading (the intake line is the page's). **Rafe Ingram has no level on the page and none is recorded for him**: the post-write audit found a named person with no level (Check #8 element 8-V) and the author accepted it on 2026-10-18, a named background messenger needing none; the realm token inside his code is the validated code's own and is not a page fact. **Surnames.** The text writes Petroc without a surname (the registry name Petroc Curnow takes the surname the author confirmed on 2026-10-17, as Orla Maddock does in Chapter 001); Ned Bolitho, Tamar Hendra and Mabyn Tippett receive their surnames only in the Grandmaster's naming of the five smallest children (Area block 06), and Brea Lawry and Silas Uren carry theirs in Area block 02. **Gender.** The page writes Berta Thaxter, Ida Norcott, Brea Lawry, Mabyn Tippett, Loveday Rowse, Dewi Moyle, Wynne Opie and Eira Sleeman as she, and Rafe Ingram, Petroc Curnow, Ned Bolitho, Silas Uren, Colan Rundle, Jenkin Lanyon and Arthen Carne as he; **Tamar Hendra's gender is not written on the page** (the build-plan role line says girl, but no pronoun is used for Tamar in the text), so it stays `OPEN-AREA` and no pronoun is canon. **Pair-Senior.** Blank for all sixteen: no formation registers such a position for them. **Limits.** Each of the sixteen speaks or does the ordinary work of a post, a bout or a bench, and any consequential act of any of them goes to the author (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4); the Gate 1 checker recorded no Tier D consequence flag (the two yields, the five's order and turns and Brea's binding are the acts the spec settled for Tier D). Two Gate 1 notes stand for the record: the Maker's lists of anchors shown were stale in six places after the editor's pass, and the Gate 1 table of 20-11 anchors by Area block supersedes them; and Ned Bolitho's code carries `B39-L01-STILL-06` as its Signature, which the page now shows only as the rocking (the unawareness clause the Gate 1 checker flagged under V9 was cut by the fix loop). Left unnamed on purpose and given no row: the teacher and the schoolchildren of Area block 01, the two men who carry the crates, the healer in the first room of Aster House and the smith whose burnt forearm she tends, the man at the rack, the older pupils of the yard who are not named (the boy and the friend who trade insults across the rings, the pupil who walks on his hands, the pupil with the rake, the voices at the rail) and the other children of the intake.

**Retrofit note, 2026-10-17 (revise-area of Chapter 001, Areas 01 to 04; Part 29 Rule 29.12.1.3, cultivation visibility).** The Current Condition cells of Ivo Lathram, Nessa Pryor, Garth Maddock, Ruan Fenner and Orla Maddock now carry the qi the rewritten text shows of each: Ivo, Nessa, Garth and Orla are unawakened and are read as absence, never as an aura, and Ruan Fenner is a Senior of the lowest sort by the miller's word. Ruan's cell also carries the softened cut (two boards of the fence split and a knock against the post of the mill-race gate, which stuck in its slot), in place of the earlier wording, which had a cut that broke the fence and struck the post. The Code cells are Baseline and were not touched; their `RLM-` tokens (`RLM-UNAWAKENED`, `RLM-SENIOR-1-LOW`) already agree with the page. The Area Maker's hand-off listed no new Tier D person for the retrofit, and no row was added.

**Canon Amendment note and revision note, 2026-10-17 (revise-chapter of Chapter 002, Ref: 1.1.1.002; Part 29 Rule 29.12.1.3, cultivation visibility; Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.6 item 5).** The text of Chapter 002 was revised in place, and the Current Condition cells of its twelve people now carry the qi the revised text shows of each. Lowen Wray, Pasco Cobb, Kitto Hale, Jowan Angove, Neot Hosken, Senara Polglase, Cador Roskilly, Newlyn Pengelly, Ebrel Tangye and Alun Tredinnick are read as absence and never as an aura (Unawakened). Enyd Carrow and Uthno Nancarrow each carry a trace, written on the page in the form ***Senior Level 1 Low***, found by Hercules's sense; nobody practises on the page. The quoted speech in the cells of Pasco Cobb, Senara Polglase, Enyd Carrow and Alun Tredinnick was re-quoted from the revised text, and the cells of Cador Roskilly and Jowan Angove were read against it. **Canon Amendment.** The Code cell is Baseline, so an alteration is a Canon Amendment (Part 37 Area 2.11), and only the author alters a token (Part 39 Rule 39.3.5.6 item 5). On the author's ruling of 2026-10-16 (cultivation visibility, with the pick of the two people delegated to the Area Maker), two tokens of two Code cells were changed: in each row the realm token from `RLM-UNAWAKENED[AI]` to `RLM-SENIOR-1-LOW[ASKED]` and the stratum token from `CC-S1[AI]` to `CC-S2[ASKED]` (the form the row of Ruan Fenner already uses). The two rows are the basket-weaver, Enyd Carrow (Area block 04), and the forester, Uthno Nancarrow (Area block 05). Both marks read `[ASKED]` because the author set the tokens. Every other token of both codes is unchanged, and the codes of the other ten people are unchanged: those ten stay Unawakened. The two changed tokens were checked here against Rule 39.3.5.5 and tests V5 and V6: Senior Level 1 Low is far below the Phase ceiling, the stratum is the one Part 16 Area 2.1 assigns to the realm, the affinity, soul rung, Heart, ideology, stability and psychology tokens stay in range for a Mortal Tier 2 realm below Demigod, and no conduct code in either row needs a realm above Unawakened or a further affinity. The Area Maker's hand-off listed no new Tier D person for the revision, so no row was added and the counts of this index are unchanged (Total Entries 45; Tier D 25).

## Part 39 — Behavioral Signature Registration

**Governing Law:** Part 39 (`character-behavioral-synthesis-law`), Areas 3, 4, 41, 45.
**Schema:** Part 37 Areas 2.1/2.2/2.3 — Character Ledger **Section 11** (Behavioral Signature, B/L) and **Section 12** (Behavioral Evolution Log, L). The character ledger template is **thirteen sections** (Section 13, Current Condition).
**Audit Hook:** Gate 1 **Check #20** (Behavioral Conformance, Part 39 Area 43) and **Check #21** (Behavioral Change Detection, Part 39 Area 44). Gate 1 runs **22** checks (Check #22, Plain Language and Narrative Impact).
**Command:** `/character-behavior <name>` runs the registration interview and writes Sections 11 and 12.

**Requirement by Part 19 tier (Part 39 Area 4.1).** Tier B is above Tier C and therefore takes the same uniform load as Tier A:

| Tier | Layers | Signatures | Contradiction pairs | Costly behavior | Stored in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Tier A** | all 36 | 12 | 2 | 1 | own `[name].md` §11–12 |
| **Tier B** | all 36 | 12 | 2 | 1 | own `[name].md` §11–12 |
| **Tier C** | mandatory-core 6 — L01 Kinesic, L03 Emotional Expression, L08 Moral Reflex, L10 Speech-Adjacent, L29 Idle/Waiting, L34 Provenance | 1 | 0 | 0 | one-paragraph behavioral line in the functional row |

Tier A and Tier B additionally carry the six usual Conditional Drift Map entries (first kill, first significant defeat, first PIS-5, next realm or tier crossing, next age-band crossing, loss of the most significant registered relationship).

**Tier D is absent from the table above by rule, not by omission.** The **Tier D — Named Conversational** tier carries **no Part 39 registration of any kind** — no Layer register, no registered Signature, no contradiction pair, no costly behavior, no drift map — and therefore has no row here; its whole record is the single index row defined in the **Tier D Named Conversational Characters — Registry** section above. **The Tier A, Tier B and Tier C rows and requirements above are unchanged by this note**, and the only Part 39 material a Tier D row may hold is the picked code of Part 39 Section 3.5. Part 19 is the single source for the Tier D rules. **Promotion out of Tier D into Tier C brings the Tier C row above into force in full**, at the moment of promotion.

**Blocking rule (Part 39 Rule 39.4.3.1):** a character may not be drafted into an Area in which they act or speak until their registration is complete. A character merely named in passing is not "acting" for this purpose.

**All fourteen side characters in this folder carry a written Part 29 Voice Register and Prosodic Signatures**, each written from an individual author interview: **Yun Ashvale, Ossian Kade, Bramwell Cog, Maren Cole, Isolde Farrow, Bastion Crowe, Selene Marrow, Edran Voss, Doran Fell, Tamsin Holt, Corin Vale, Brannic Ost, Vaneth** and **Kellan Rothe.** Together with the eight heroes this completes the **full cast of 22**. **Per-character status is authoritative in that character's §1, never here.**

**Wulfric Rask (registered 2026-10-08; a Tier B villain since 2026-10-18, his file moved to `ledger-character-villain/`) is not one of the fourteen side characters:** his Part 29 Voice Register was set by the author's ruling of 2026-10-18 (assertive and command-oriented, flowing and not abrupt; his thought under the same register), and its two prosodic determinants (menu items 1 and 5) are the recorder's reading of those words, flagged for author confirmation (`ledger-character-villain/wulfric-rask.md` §1).

**Max Pryce (Tier B, registered 2026-10-17) is not one of the fourteen either:** his Part 29 Voice Register is set by the author's own answer at the Tier B interview of 2026-10-17 (question 16), for the practice floor only: PSM-1 (rolling sentences) and PSM-7 (sword-work words first, plain speech when the exchange is social). It carries a Tier B scope, and nothing outside the practice floor is registered (`max-pryce.md` §1). *(Evolution Entry 1, 2026-10-20, author ruling 2026-10-20: the scope also covers his talk as an older-pupil helper in the cultivation classroom of Hall 5; the sentence above is the base, `max-pryce.md` §1 and Section 14.)*

**Gunnar Stavik (Tier B, registered 2026-10-22) is not one of the fourteen either:** his Part 29 Voice Register is set by the author's own answer at the Tier B interview of 2026-10-22 (question 13), for his office and craft only (the watch order, the count, the handover, the report to the Court and to the Marshal, the road and the mess table; courtship, philosophy and speech about his own grief are unregistered): PSM-1 (plain sentences of middle length in company, long formal clauses ending in the witness list when he constitutes an act), PSM-5 (blunt on what he saw, bounded on causes) and PSM-6 (no epigram). It carries a Tier B scope, and nothing outside it is registered (`gunnar-stavik.md` §1).

**Magnus Dalsen (Tier B, registered 2026-10-22) is not one of the fourteen either:** his Part 29 Voice Register is set by the author's own answer at the Tier B interview of 2026-10-22 (question 12), for the Host's day only (hearing a report, the question put after it, the ask, the held pause and the plain statement of fact; a muster order, a ceremony speech, talk of the sovereigns and private talk are unregistered): PSM-1 (full connected sentences of middle length, each with one clause of reason), PSM-5 (blunt on fact, and framed as "what I would ask" on force) and no epigram budget. It carries a Tier B scope, and nothing outside it is registered (`magnus-dalsen.md` §1). The page of Chapter 009 (Ref: 1.1.1.009), Area block 08, is his first speech.

Layer L35 is retired; no pair or comparison is recorded.

## Part 28 Area 17 — Lore Section Schema (Character Domain — Side Character)

**Governing Law:** Part 28 Area 17 (Lore Creation Law). **Audit Hook:** ALCA dimension AL-10 (`guardrails/audit/guardrail-alca.md`) (Lore Completeness Alignment).

**Cross-Reference Alignment:** Per Part 28 Area 17.2 (Character) and Part 19: Side characters follow the **four-tier Depth Spectrum (A/B/C/D)**. Each tier has a different depth ceiling — the Depth Test applies at each tier's own level, not universally.

**Per-Tier Axis Requirements:**

**Tier A (full ledger file, thirteen-section template, Section 3 includes Innate Moves subsection):**
- Sections 1–13 populated (Section 13 reads `none recorded at story start` until an Area changes it).
- Depth Test: "Can a pacing-appropriate immersion scene be written from only this ledger?"
- Additional: Narrative Function (what structural role this character plays — mentor / foil / catalyst / guardian / traitor-in-waiting) + Termination Plan (how and when this arc is expected to close, per Part 19 Rule 19.9.3.2).
- **Section 3 Innate Moves subsection (Part 37 Area 2.1, Part 6 Area 12):** required in every Tier A and Tier B side character ledger file. Non-species-innate characters record "(none registered — character is non-species-innate type)". Hybrid sentient side characters (Part 16 Area 17), bipedal sentient species members (Part 16 Area 18), or other sentient beings (Part 16 Area 19) register S-3 and S-8-with-S-3 moves per Part 6 Area 12.3.5.
- **Species Classification (Part 16 Areas 17–19):** if applicable, the Tier A side character's species designator, SADS position, and species-cultural affiliations are recorded in Section 1/Section 2 per Part 16 Area 18 and extended character ledger schema.

**Tier B (own full ledger file; the registry row in this index points at it):**
- Name, Narrative Function, Current Status, Key Relationship to Hero, CS%/TS% (if narratively tracked), at least one named defining trait.
- **Species-Innate Summary field:** if the Tier B character is a species-innate-capable entity (bipedal sentient, hybrid sentient, other sentient being per Part 16 Areas 17–19), a single-line summary of their most-used S-3 move and its anatomical derivation. Otherwise omit.
- Depth Test: "Can this character execute their narrative function in a scene from only this ledger?"

**Tier C (single-line index row):**
- Name, Role/Function, Status.
- Depth Test not required; Tier C characters are functional background.

**Promotion Rule:** Promotion of a Tier B or Tier C person follows Part 19 Rule 19.9.2.1 and Part 39 Rule 39.4.3.2; the author rules (Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4).

**Silent Overwrite Prohibition (Part 28 Area 17.5):** Once side character lore is recorded at any tier, it is fixed under the Establishment Threshold.
